{D257}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya sUr+ya gar+b+ha nA ma ma hA bai bu l+yaM sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i sde nyi ma'i snying po zhes bya ba'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus 
[\[91b.2\]]gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa na bzhugs te/_gang dag phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan grangs med dpag tu med pa dag nas der lhags pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po grangs med/_dpag tu med/_
[\[91b.3\]]brjod du med pa dag gis yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas so/_/de bzhin du phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dang gzhan dag nas der 'dus te lhags pa'i nyan thos chen po grangs med/_dpag tu med/_brjod du med pa dag gis kyang yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis 
[\[91b.4\]]bltas so/_/de bzhin du phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dang gzhan dag nas 'dir lhags pa brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa'i dbang po dang /_lha'i dbang po dang /_klu'i dbang po dang /_gnod sbyin gyi dbang po dang /_dri za'i dbang po dang /_lha ma yin gyi dbang po dang /_nam 
[\[91b.5\]]mkha' lding gi dbang po dang /_mi 'am ci'i dbang po dang /_lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po grangs med/_dpag tu med/_brjod du med pa dag gis kyang mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad sa dang nam mkha' la 'gro ba'i lam gang bar gyur to/_/de bzhin du 'dod pa na spyod pa dang /_
[\[91b.6\]]gzugs na spyod pa'i lha gzhan dang gzhan dag dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po gzhan dang /_gzhan grangs med dpag tu med pa dag kyang lhags nas/_bcom ldan 'das la gyen du lta 
[\[91b.7\]]zhing /_bcom ldan 'das ring po mi thogs par mthar phyin pa/_nam mkha' ltar snang ba/_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rab tu 'byed pa/_dbugs rngub pa dang /_'byung ba dran pa 'chi ba med pa'i sgo/_tshangs pa'i gnas pa lung bstan pa ston pa na cang mi smra bar 'khod de thams 
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]cad dang ldan pa'i mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad sa dang nam mkha'i lam gang bar gyur cing /_da dung bcom ldan 'das kyi chos kyi char gyi ston pas tshim par ma gyur nas thal mo dag rab tu sbyar te/_bcom ldan 'das kho na la blta zhing 'khod pa ni dper na 
[\[92a.2\]]nad pa dag sman pa la lta bar byed pa bzhin dang /_mun pas khebs pa dag snang ba la lta bar byed pa bzhin dang /_chu bos khyer ba dag chu 'gram du lta bar byed pa bzhin dang /_sdug bsngal bar gyur pa dag skyabs la lta bar byed pa bzhin te/_de ltar mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[92a.3\]]zhing thams cad sa dang nam mkha'i lam byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dag dang /_nyan thos dag dang /_dbang po brgya byin dag nas lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po dag gi bar dang /_'dod pa na spyod pa dang /_gzugs na spyod pa'i lha gzhan dang gzhan dag nas mi dang 
[\[92a.4\]]mi ma yin pa rnams gzhan dang gzhan dag gi bar bcom ldan 'das chos dang ldan pa chos kyi rgyal po la lta zhing thal mo rab tu sbyar te cang mi smra bar 'khod par gyur to/_/de'i tshe tshangs pa chen po pad ma 'bar ba'i dpal zhes bya ba sangs rgyas mang po la lhag par bya ba byas pa dge 
[\[92a.5\]]ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa/_bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog pa/_chos la dmigs pa'i byams pa shin tu yongs su sbyang ba byas par gyur to/_/de nas tshangs pa chen po pad ma 'bar ba'i dpal stan las langs te/_bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas 
[\[92a.6\]]pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/_bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa brjod pa 'dis chos bshad par gsol ba btab bo/_/ring du gshegs shing myur ba'i rgyun dang ldan/_/zad pa lhur mdzad 
[\[92a.7\]]mi g.yo 'od gsal ba/_/rtogs pa thams cad skyed par mdzad pa po/_/khyod gsung rab zhi rgyu skar tshul mkhyen mkhas/_/dbugs rngub pa dang 'byung ba dran pa mkhyen/_/tshangs pa'i gnas pa bzhi po dag dang ldan/_/srid pa gsum po ji ltar dag 'gyur bar/_/sems can 'gro 
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]ba spong bar mdzad pa po/_/tshul gcig ldan pa nyid kyi mkhyen mnga' ba/_/lha mi rnam pa mang po khri dag kyang /_/som nyi rgal mdzad dge brnyes chu bo rgal/_/bde 'gro'i mkhyen pa myur dang ldan pa po/_/byang chub spyod dang sangs rgyas chos mngon sum/_/sems g.yo 
[\[92b.2\]]sems can kha cig 'dod klung lhung /_/mun pas khebs shing mig dang mi ldan pa'i/_/sdug bsngal thams cad bcom par gyur lags pa/_/ma thos dge bral slob dpon mkhas ldan pa'i/_/skye bo bral la mi dbang thugs rje skyed/_/mnyam pa'i chos nyan skye ba'i pha rol 
[\[92b.3\]]mchi/_/srid pa gcod pa bshad par mdzad du gsol/_/skye klung pha rol gshegs thub thugs rjes gsungs/_/blo 'chal skye mang sred pa'i dra ba'i lam/_/de dag thams cad nyon mongs rtsa ba can/_/der ni sems can 'khyams 'gyur gcad du gsol/_/gang du sdug 
[\[92b.4\]]bsngal rab zhi dbang drug dang /_/yid kyang zhi 'gyur sred skyon rgyal ba gsung /_/sred pa bral ba sred rgyu mi mnga' ba/_/sems can gdung ba dpal kun bral gyur pa/_/de slad thub pa thugs brtse ba yi rgyus/_/sred pa yi ni chu klung skems mdzad pa/_/myur du bde zhing 'chi 
[\[92b.5\]]ba ma mchis pa'i/_/chos kyang sred pa gcod pa bshad par gsol/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa pad ma 'bar ba'i dpal la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/gang dag sngon chad pha rol phyin/_/spyod pa spyad cing spyod 'gyur ba/_/de dag nyan thos chos rnams la/_/
[\[92b.6\]]nam yang dga' bar mi bya ste/_/sangs rgyas chos las nyams 'gyur ba'i/_/ltung ba gnyis pa gzhan med de/_/de ni yang dang yang du yang /_/gnyi gar sems ni 'jug pa yin/_/khro dang nyan thos chos rnams dang /_/rkyen gyi byang chub 'jug pa yi/_/chos gsum rnam par 
[\[92b.7\]]spangs pas na/_/sangs rgyas chos rnams rdzogs par 'gyur/_/skye gnas bzhi ldan sems can 'di/_/sangs rgyas zhing 'dir lhags pa dag_/thams cad 'dod pa rten mi byed/_/thams cad mi sdug thob pa yin/_/'di na sems can mkhas pa gang /_/byang chub 
[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]mchog gi phyir zhugs shing /_/gang blo gzungs dang bzod pa la/_/brtson zhing legs par goms byas la/_/dbang po drug gi sgo bsdams shing /_/skyil mo krung gis dran gzhag nas/_/rang gi spyod yul sa non pa/_/phyi ma'i dus kyi de bzhin gshegs/_/
[\[93a.2\]]nor bdun rab tu rdzogs pa'ang yin/_/'dir ni mi sdug tshul bshad na/_/kun gzhi gang yin sems can gyi/_/'ching ba brtan pa 'dir bcad 'gyur/_/ngas ni chos kyi ser sna spangs/_/sems can srung phyir ma bshad de/_/'di la byang chub sems dpa' yis/_/byang 
[\[93a.3\]]chub phyir sems 'dir ma btang /_/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas de dag gis/_/chos 'di yun ring gnas bya zhes/_/sa 'di yongs su spyad mdzad cing /_/sa 'di byin gyis brlabs pa yin/_/de bzhin du ni khyod kyis kyang /_/zhing 'di la ni rtse bar byos/_/de bzhin bzod pa 
[\[93a.4\]]dag gis rtses/_/bsdams nas yun ring gnas par byos/_/nyan thos theg pa ngas bshad de/_/sems can chags las thar bya zhing /_/phung po dag dang khams rnams dang /_/skye mched stong pa ngas bshad de/_/zhi gnas rnam pa bcu gsum dang /_/lhag mthong nga yis 
[\[93a.5\]]bshad bya ste/_/'dod pa'i 'ching bas dam bcings pa'i/_/sems can de yis rnam dag 'gyur/_/sems can rnams la snying brtse bas/_/nyan thos rnams kyi bden pa bzhi/_/chos rnams rtag tu yun gnas dang /_/zag pa med phyir ngas bshad do/_/nyan thos bden pas 
[\[93a.6\]]'dul ba ni/_/srid pa'i mtsho las ngas bsgral lo/_/byang chub spyod pa thams cad kyang /_/phyi nas khyed la bstan par bya'o/_/de nas de'i tshe thams cad dang ldan pa'i mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams 'di snyam du sems te/_kye ma da ltar de bzhin gshegs 
[\[93a.7\]]pa 'di ni nyan thos kyi gtam yang dag par rab tu ston par mdzad kyi/_theg pa chen po'i gtam ni mi mdzad do snyam mo/_/de nas de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad 'di snyam du sems te/_kye ma da ltar de bzhin gshegs pa 'di ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'dir sa yongs su spyad pa'i 
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]phyir dang /_ma 'ongs pa'i dus na chos kyi tshul ring du gnas par bya ba'i phyir dang /_da ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'dir gang dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung rgyun mi 'chad par bya ba'i phyir dang /_ma 'ongs pa'i dus na lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dang /_lha ma yin dang /_
[\[93b.2\]]nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po dang /_mi dang /_mi ma yin pa ma dad pa rnams mngon par dad par bya ba'i phyir dang /_dad pa rnams phyir zhing 'phel bar bya ba'i phyir dang /_sems can chos la mngon par brtson pa dag dang /_chos la gnas pa dag dang /_
[\[93b.3\]]yon tan gyis phyug pa dag dang /_tshul khrims kyi dri dang yang dag par ldan pa rnams bde ba la reg pa dang /_rnam par tho 'tsham pa med par bya ba'i phyir dang /_byang grol gyi bde ba yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir dang /_ji nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di shin tu yon tan dag gis 
[\[93b.4\]]bsgo bar bya ba'i phyir dang /_phyogs bcu na gnas pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan thams cad kyang bsgo ba'i 'os su gyur pa dang /_bla ma la gus pa dang /_phyag bya ba'i 'os su gyur par bya ba'i phyir bdag cag nang du yang dag par 'jog par bzhed pas/_ma la bdag cag gis 
[\[93b.5\]]da de bzhin gshegs pa'i dgongs pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ste/_bdag cag thams cad kyis sa'i steng du skyil mo krung gis 'khod par bya zhing /_rang rang gi rnam pa sna tshogs kyi mchog thams cad dang ldan pa'i bzod pa dang /_gzungs dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_rnal 'byor 
[\[93b.6\]]dag gis gnas pa dag tu bya gor ma chag snyam mo/_/de nas de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag bskal pa brgyas nges par byung ba dag dang /_gang dag sa bcu dag dang /_gang dag mi skye ba'i chos kyi bzod pa la gnas pa'i bar dag dang /_gang dag sangs 
[\[93b.7\]]rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad la gzhan la rag ma las pa'i ye shes la mkhas pa dag dang /_mi 'jigs pa thob pa dag gi bar de dag thams cad sa steng 'dir skyil mo krung gis 'khod par gyur to/_/de bzhin du rang rang gi rnam pa sna tshogs kyi mchog thams cad kyi bzod pa 
[\[94a\]]
[\[94a.1\]]dang /_gzungs dang /_ting nge 'dzin dag la mnyam par gzhag pa dag tu gyur to/_/yang de'i tshe bsod nams kyi stobs ji lta ba bzhin du rgyu 'thun pa'i rgyu'i stobs kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mnyam par gzhog pa de dag dang /_byang chub 
[\[94a.2\]]sems dpa' chen po bsam gtan byed pa de dag las kha cig gi lus las 'od 'di lta bu byung ste/_dper na mar me'i 'od lta bu'o/_/kha cig gi ni skar mda' dang mnyam pa dag go/_/kha cig gi ni brgya byin dang mnyam pa dag go/_/kha cig gi ni mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa 
[\[94a.3\]]dang mnyam pa nas kha cig gi ni zla ba dang mnyam pa'i bar dag go/_/kha cig gi ni nyi ma dang mnyam pa dag go/_/kha cig gi ni nyi ma gnyis dang mnyam pa dag go/_/kha cig gi ni nyi ma gsum dang mnyam pa dag go/_/kha cig gi ni nyi ma lnga dang mnyam pa dag go/_/kha cig gi ni 
[\[94a.4\]]nyi ma stong dang mnyam pa'i bar dag go/_/kha cig gi ni lus las ni nyi ma bye ba brgya stong dang mnyam pa'i 'od dag byung ste/_mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di thams cad sngon ma mthong ma thos pa'i snang ba chen po rgya che ba de lta bus 'jig rten khyab par gyur to/_/de dag gi lus 
[\[94a.5\]]las mchog tu sems can gzhan dag gi lus dang sems kyi bde ba la reg pa dang /_dga' ba dang /_rab tu tshim par byed pa/_nad thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa/_lta ba ngan pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa/_lus kyi gnas ngan len thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed 
[\[94a.6\]]pa/_bkres pa dang skom pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa/_'dod pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa/_khro ba thams cad dang /_'jigs pa thams cad dang /_nyon mongs pa dang /_'ching ba thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i 'od byung ste/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na srid 
[\[94a.7\]]pa gsum gyi btson ra'i sdug bsngal gyi 'jigs pa thams cad kyi bar rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na sems can thams cad sangs rgyas la 'bab pa dag dang /_chos la 'bab pa dag dang /_dge 'dun gyi yon tan la dga' ba la mkhas pa dag 'khod 
[\[94b\]]
[\[94b.1\]]par gyur to/_/yang de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di phyogs bcu'i gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong pa dang /_stong pa ma yin pa dag dang /_yongs su dag pa dang /_yongs su dag pa ma yin pa dag na kun nas lham me/_lhan ne/_lhang nger yang dag par 
[\[94b.2\]]snang ba ni 'di lta ste/_mtshan mo mun pa byung ba na zla ba dang /_nyi ma brgya stong shar nas lham me/_lhan ne/_lhang nge ba bzhin te/_de ltar de'i tshe mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing yang phyogs bcu'i gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong pa dang /_stong pa ma yin pa dag 
[\[94b.3\]]dang /_yongs su dag pa dang /_yongs su ma dag pa dag na kun nas mchog tu lham me/_lhan ne/_lhang nger gyur to/_/de nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing de dang de dag na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag yod pa dang /_gang dag bskal pa brgyas nges par byung ba dag 
[\[94b.4\]]dang /_gang dag sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad la gzhan la rag ma las pa'i ye shes la mkhas pa dag dang /_mi 'jigs pa thob pa'i bar de dag sangs rgyas kyi mthus de nas chas te/_sems kyi skad cig ma gcig dang ldan pas sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'dir zhugs 
[\[94b.5\]]nas gnas ji lta ba bzhin sa steng du skyil mo krung gis 'khod par gyur to/_/de bzhin du rang rang gi dge ba'i phyogs la nan tan dag tu byed cing ji ltar dge ba'i phyogs kyi stobs bskyed pa thob pa de dag dang /_mi 'jigs pa'i rgyu dang rkyen dang ldan pa de dag gi lus las de 'dra ba kho 
[\[94b.6\]]na'i 'od byung bar gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi dge 'dun gyis yongs su bskor nas nyan thos chen po rnams la bka' stsal pa/_khyed bsam gtan gyi yul gyi mthu la ltos shig_/byin gyi rlabs kyi rgyus bdud thams cad kyi yul dang /_klu thams cad kyi 
[\[94b.7\]]yul zil gyis mnan nas/_sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis 'dir chos kyi tshul dang /_dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung rgyun mi 'chad pa'i phyir dang /_gsal bar mdzad pa'i phyir mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di thams cad yongs su spyad pa mdzad do/_/de bzhin 
[\[95a\]]
[\[95a.1\]]du da ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po grangs med pa/_dpag tu med pa gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed bsam gtan dang /_mngon par shes pa'i yul la rnam par rtse ba dang ldan pa dag gis mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na chos kyi tshul yun ring du 
[\[95a.2\]]gnas par bya ba'i phyir dang /_gsal bar bya ba'i phyir dang /_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po dang /_mi dang /_mi ma yin pa ma dad pa rnams dad par bya ba'i phyir dang /_dad pa rnams phyir zhing 
[\[95a.3\]]'phel bar bya ba'i phyir dang /_sems can chos la mngon par brtson pa dag dang /_chos kyis gnas pa dag dang /_yon tan gyis phyug pa dag dang /_sems la dbang dang ldan par gyur pa rnams bde ba la reg pa dang /_rnam par tho 'tsham pa med par bya ba'i phyir dang /_nga'i bstan 
[\[95a.4\]]pa rab tu mi 'jig par bya ba'i phyir dang /_byang grol gyi bde ba yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir sa chen po 'di yongs su spyad par byas pa yin no/_/don de nyid byin gyis brlab par bya ba'i phyir de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams bsam gtan gyi yul dag gis 
[\[95a.5\]]rnam par rtse bar byed do/_/sa phyogs gang dang gang na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'di dag 'khod cing bsam gtan gyi yul dag gis rnam par rtsen par byed pa de dang der da ltar dang /_ma 'ongs pa'i dus na yang kha cig tu la lar nga 'das nas sems can 'khor ba'i 
[\[95a.6\]]'jigs pas skrag par byed cing mya ngan las 'das pa 'dod pa dag sku gdung gi mchod rten dag byed du 'jug par 'gyur zhing /_sangs rgyas kyi bzhugs khang dag gam/_sangs rgyas kyi sku gzugs dag gam/_chos kyi bzhugs khang dag byed par 'gyur ro/_/yang na der glegs 
[\[95a.7\]]bam du bri ba byas nas nga'i chos kyi sku yang 'chang bar 'gyur ro/_/der yang mchod pa rnam pa sna tshogs dag kyang byed par 'gyur zhing /_der rin po che rnam pa sna tshogs dag kyang 'bul bar 'gyur te/_'di lta ste/_gser dang /_dngul dang /_bai dUr+ya dang /_shel dang /_mu tig 
[\[95b\]]
[\[95b.1\]]dmar po dang /_rdo'i snying po dang /_spug dang /_nor bu dang /_mu tig dang /_dbyig dang /_dung dang /_man shel dang /_byi ru la sogs pa dang /_dar yug dang /_dril bu dang /_za 'ug dang /_gso ras bzang po dang /_yul ka shi'i ras bzang po dang /_dbu stan nang tshangs 
[\[95b.2\]]can dang /_gding ba dang /_gdugs dang /_rgyal mtshan dang /_ba dan dang /_chos gos dang /_mgur gyi rgyan dang /_phyag gi rgyan dang /_spos dang /_me tog dang /_bdug pa dang /_rol mo'i sgra dang /_lus 'dud pa dang /_bla ma dang /_gus pa chen po dang /_gces par 
[\[95b.3\]]byed pa dag tu 'gyur ro/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe phyag sor bcu'i thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/de dag gi mchod pa ni de nas nga nyid kyis bzhes par mdzad de/_sems can de dag thams cad ni bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du theg pa gsum gyis phyir mi 
[\[95b.4\]]ldog pa dag tu 'gyur ro/_/sa phyogs la lar ni gtsug lag khang dang /_gnas khang dang /_'chag sa dang /_dgon pa'i gnas dang /_skyed mos tshal dang /_kun dga'i ra ba dag rab tu shom par byed do/_/yang na nyan thos kyi chos kyi gnas pa rnams la mchod pa dang /_
[\[95b.5\]]rim gro rnam pa sna tshogs 'di lta ste/_chos gos dang /_bsod snyoms dang /_mal cha dang /_stan dang /_na ba'i gsos sman dang /_yo byad dag gis byed par 'gyur ba'am/_byed du 'jug par 'gyur ro/_/gtsug lag khang dang /_'chag sa dang /_dgon pa'i gnas

[\[95b.7\]]su gtong bar 'gyur te/_'di lta ste/_zhing gi gzhi 'am/_khyim gyi gzhi'am/_kun dga'i ra ba'i gzhi'am/_bran dang /_bran mo dang /_glang po che dang /_rta dang /_rnga mo dang /_ba lang dang /_ma he dang /_bong bu dang /_phyugs gzhan dag dang /_snod spyad kyi yo byad rnam 
[\[96a\]]
[\[96a.1\]]pa sna tshogs dag der yongs su gtong bar 'gyur zhing /_nga'i nyan thos kyis chos kyi gnas pa rnams longs spyod par bya ba'i phyir mal cha dang /_stan gyi yo byad rnam pa sna tshogs dag sems kyi rgyan gyi don dang /_sems kyi yo byad kyi don dang /_rnal 'byor gyi tshogs 
[\[96a.2\]]kyi don dang /_byang grol gyi bde ba don gyi mchog thob par bya ba'i phyir sngo ba dag tu byed do/_/da ltar dang ma 'ongs pa'i dus na nga'i bstan pa la phan gdags pa'i phyir yongs su gtong ba rnam pa sna tshogs dag yongs su gtong bar byed do/_/de bzhin du sems can de dag gi sbyin pa 
[\[96a.3\]]las byung ba'i bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po de dag thams cad la ci nas kyang sems can de dag 'bras bu chen por 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir nga rjes su thugs rangs par mdzad do/_/bzhes par mdzad do/_/sems can log par ltung ba'i chos can ma yin pa ngan 'gro gsum rnam 
[\[96a.4\]]par spangs pa de dag thams cad bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du theg pa gsum gyis phyir mi ldog pa dag tu 'gyur ro/_/sems can de dag yun ji srid du 'khor bar 'khor bar 'gyur ba de srid du yang longs spyod gnyis po chos kyi longs spyod dang /_zang zing gi longs spyod dang mi 
[\[96a.5\]]'bral bar 'gyur ro/_/ri dang /_nags khrod dang /_shing ljon pa chen po dang /_dgon pa dang /_nags 'dab gang du yang rung ste/_da ltar dang ma 'ongs pa'i dus na thar pa don du gnyer ba chos kyis gnas pa'i dge slong dang /_dge slong ma dang /_dge bsnyen dang /_dge bsnyen 
[\[96a.6\]]ma sgra bskyungs pa dag dang /_gcom bskyungs pa dag dang /_nang du yang dag 'jog gi tshul dang bcas pa dag dang /_tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa dang 'tsham pa dag 'byung bar 'gyur zhing /_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dag kyang gnas par 'gyur ro/_/dge slong dag gdul 
[\[96a.7\]]ba'i don du bsam gtan gyi yul gyi mthu la ltos shig_/bdud thams cad kyi yul dang /_klu thams cad kyi yul zil gyis mnan nas/_sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs te 'tsho zhing gzhes pa rnams kyis chos kyi tshul ring du gnas par bya ba'i phyir dang /_dkon mchog 
[\[96b\]]
[\[96b.1\]]gsum gyi gdung rgyun mi gcad pa'i phyir sngon yang mi mjed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di yongs su spyod pa mdzad do/_/da ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po grangs med pa/_dpag tu med pa gang dag phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan gang gA'i klung gi 
[\[96b.2\]]bye ma snyed nas nga'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'dir zhu ba dang /_zhal blta ba dang /_phyag 'tshal ba dang /_mchod pa dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba'i phyir dang /_nam mkha'i mig dang ldan pa/_so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi dang ldan pa/_chos kyis gnas pa rnams kyi byang chub sems 
[\[96b.3\]]dpa'i spyod pa mnyan pa'i phyir lhags pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag kyang nga la snying nye ba'i phyir chos kyi tshul ring du gnas par bya ba'i don dang /_dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung rgyun mi gcad pa'i don du bsam gtan gyi yul gyis sa steng du 'khod nas/_
[\[96b.4\]]bdud thams cad kyi yul tshar bcad pa'i phyir dang /_'khor bas skrag pa sbyin pa la mngon par brtson pa'i sems can de dag la snying brtse ba'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di yongs su spyod pa dag tu byed do/_/da ltar dang ma 'ongs pa'i dus na nga'i bstan pa 'di dang /_dam pa'i 
[\[96b.5\]]chos sam/_dam pa'i chos kyi gzugs brnyan yun ji srid gnas par chos kyis gnas pa'i dge slong rnams la khyim dang /_kun dga'i ra ba dang /_zhing dang /_bran dang /_bran mo'i bar gang su dag gis de ltar bsngos pa de la sdig pa spyod pa'i dge slong tshul khrims 'chal 
[\[96b.6\]]pa gang dag tha na 'dab ma gcig dang /_'bras bu gcig dang /_me tog gcig cig yongs su spyod pa dag tu byed na de ni mi blun po de la don ma yin pa dang /_phan pa ma yin par 'gyur te/_de la gnod pa bzhi yang 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_phyogs thams cad 
[\[96b.7\]]du mi snyan pa chen po 'gro ba yin/_mdza' bo dang /_nye du dang /_snag gi gnyen mtshams dang /_mkhan po dang /_slob dpon dang /_rim gro pa sdug cing yid du 'ong ba dag dang myur du 'bral bar 'gyur ba yin/_nad tshabs chen po yun ring ba dag gis thebs par 'gyur ba 
[\[97a\]]
[\[97a.1\]]yin/_de'i chos gos dang lhung bzed gang yod pa yang 'jigs pa chen po lngas phrogs par 'gyur ba yin no/_/lnga gang dag gis zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rgyal po dang /_chom rkun dang /_me dang /_chu dang /_bgo skal la spyod pa mi mdza' ba dang lnga po de dag gis 
[\[97a.2\]]so/_/de'i 'jig rten pha rol gyi 'dod pa ma yin pa'i 'bras bu bzhi myur du 'grub par 'gyur ba yin te/_bzhi gang dag yin zhe na/_lus zhig nas sems can dmyal ba chen po mi bzad par skye bar 'gyur ba yin/_de gal te brgya la res 'ga' zhig dus ring zhig 'das nas sems 
[\[97a.3\]]can dmyal ba de nas shi 'phos nas 'brog dgon pa ro skam gyi nang du dud 'gro dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]kyi ris su long ba dang rkang lag med pa dag tu skye bar 'gyur zhing /_de lo brgya stong mang po dag tu bkres pa dang /_skom pas yongs su gdung ba chen po'i sdug bsngal nyams su myong bar 
[\[97a.4\]]'gyur ba yin/_gal te brgya la res 'ga' zhig dus ring zhig 'das nas de dag shi 'phos nas lo brgya stong mang po dag tu sbrul gdug pa long ba rdul za ba dag gi nang du skye bar 'gyur ba yin/_de brgya la res 'ga' zhig de nas shi 'phos nas gal te mi rnams kyi nang du skyes na/_'on 
[\[97a.5\]]kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing snyigs ma lnga dang ldan pa stong pa dag tu mi nyid 'thob pa rnyed par 'gyur zhing /_mong rtul ba dang /_tshul du mi shes par 'gyur ba dang /_mig ma tshang ba dang /_lag pa ma tshang ba dang /_rkang pa ma tshang ba dang /_khyi'i longs spyod kyi kha zas la gdu zhing[(]rkyag [,]skyag [)] khung sa'i 
[\[97a.6\]]kha zas za ba dang /_'dam rdzab dang /_phyag dar khrod na nyal ba dang /_'brog dang /_dgon pa dang /_lam po che rnams na 'tsho bar 'gyur la/_de de nas shi 'phos nas kyang yang sems can dmyal ba rnams kyi nang du skye bar 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir de'i 'bras bu 'dod pa ma yin 
[\[97a.7\]]pa de ltar lci ba 'byung zhe na/_'di ltar sdig pa spyod pa'i dge slong de ni 'di ltar chos kyis gnas pa rnams la khyim dang /_zhing dang /_kun dga'i ra ba dang /_bran dang /_bran mo dang /_phyugs dag gi bar bsngos pa dag phrogs nas gang zag rang gi yongs su longs spyod pas 
[\[97b\]]
[\[97b.1\]]longs spyod par byed pa'i phyir te/_sdig pa spyod pa'i dge slong de lta bu de ni de ltar ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'byung ba thams cad kyi chos kyi tshul med par bya ba dang /_rnam par gzhig pa dang /_rgyun gcad pa dang /_nub par bya ba'i phyir brtson pa yin 
[\[97b.2\]]no/_/de bzhin du dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung med par bya ba nas nub par bya ba'i bar du brtson pa yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di ltar dge ba'i phyogs ni tshogs dang ldan pa yin la/_chos kyi tshul dang dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung yang dge ba'i phyogs kyis rab tu phye ba 
[\[97b.3\]]yin te/_de'i phyir de nyid kyi mi 'dod pa'i 'bras bu rnam par smin pa lci ba mngon par 'grub pa yin no/_/de nas gnas brtan ga ya 'od srung gis mchi ma blags nas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das mi nyid rnyed pa ni ma thob kyang mi bsngo'i/_rab tu 
[\[97b.4\]]byung nas bza' ba dang btung ba'i slad du log par sgrub pa ni de lta ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_ga ya 'od srung chen po de de bzhin no/_/ji skad smras pa de bzhin te/_gang du de'i sngon gyi las rnams zad pa dag tu 'gyur ba'i sems can dmyal 
[\[97b.5\]]bar gnas pa ni sla'i/_'di na gang gzhan du spyod pa ni de lta ma yin no/_/'on kyang skyes bu blun po kha cig mi nyid thob pa rnyed par dka' ba rnyed de legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba gang la sdug bsngal thams cad zad pa dang /_zag pa thams cad mthar thug par 'gyur 
[\[97b.6\]]ba ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i rab tu 'byung ba rnyed par dka' ba dang /_bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos po thob nas 'on kyang skyes bu blun po de dag gang gis mchod par mi 'gyur ba dang /_bza' btung dang bral bar 'gyur ba mi dge ba'i phyogs rnal 'byor yid la byed 
[\[97b.7\]]pa yongs su bsams nas rgyal rigs dang /_bram ze dang /_rje'u rigs dang /_dmangs rigs dad pa can dag gis khyim dang /_zhing dang /_kun dga'i ra ba dang /_skyed mos tshal dang /_bran dang /_bran mo nas rkang bzhi pa dag gi bar dge slong chos kyis gnas pa/_
[\[98a\]]
[\[98a.1\]]yon tan gyis phyug pa dag longs spyod par bya ba'i phyir dang /_dge ba'i phyogs dang 'thun par bya ba'i phyir de ltar bsngos pa la sdig pa spyod pa'i dge slong de mang du thos pa'i mthu'am/_khyim na gnas pa'i mthus de dag la bsngos pa'i longs spyod dang /_nye bar 
[\[98a.2\]]spyod pa phrogs nas bdag nyid kyis gang zag gi 'du shes kyis yongs su spyod par byed de/_skyes bu blun po de'i log par bsgrub pa de ni de lta bur 'gyur ro/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la rgyal po gzugs can snying pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das gang 
[\[98a.3\]]dge slong chos kyis gnas pa/_yon tan gyis phyug pa dag la de ltar bsngos pa'i longs spyod dang /_nye bar spyod pa phrogs nas bdag nyid kyis yongs su spyod par bgyid pa'i dge slong rab tu byung ba'i mi 'dod pa'i 'bras bu de lta bu 'byung lags na/_dpe tsam du bgyi na gang 
[\[98a.4\]]yang khyim na gnas par gyur la/_dge slong chos kyis gnas pa rnams la de ltar bsngos pa'i longs spyod dang /_nye bar spyod pa phrogs nas bdag nyid kyis yongs su spyod par bgyid na de bsod nams ma lags pa'i phung po ji snyed cig dang ldan par 'gyur ba lags/_bcom ldan 'das 
[\[98a.5\]]kyis bka' stsal pa/_rgyal po chen po thong khyod kyis smras pa des ci zhig bya/_gsol pa/_btsun pa bcom ldan 'das dge slong chos kyis gnas pa/_yon tan gyis phyug pa de lta bu dag la longs spyod dang nye bar spyod pa dag gis stsol bar ni bgyid kyi/_'phrog par ni mi bgyid 
[\[98a.6\]]lags so/_/'on kyang slad ma'i dus na rgyal rigs dang /_bram ze dang /_rje'u rigs dang /_dmangs rigs ma dad pa 'jig rten pha rol gyis 'jigs par mi lta ba dag 'byung bar 'gyur zhing /_dge slong chos kyis gnas pa/_yon tan gyis phyug pa dag la de ltar bsngos 
[\[98a.7\]]pa'i longs spyod dang /_nye bar spyod pa phrogs nas bdag nyid kyis yongs su spyod par bgyid pa gang dag lags pa de dag gi rgud par 'gyur ba gang lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rgyal po chen po gal te ngas lung bong lta bu'i rgyal rigs dag nas/_dmangs 
[\[98b\]]
[\[98b.1\]]rigs dag gi bar dag chos kyis gnas pa yon tan gyis phyug pa dag la de ltar bsngos pa'i longs spyod dang /_nye bar spyod pa phrogs nas bdag nyid kyis longs spyod par byed pa gang dag yin pa de dag gi log par sgrub pa 'byung ba de bshad na ni/_de'i tshe de dag kyang nga'i 
[\[98b.2\]]bka' bstan pa'i phrin las kyi skabs thob nas spong ba dag tu byed la yid ches pa dag tu mi byed de/_de dag ni phung bar 'gyur ba'i 'jigs pa chen po'i gnod pa gnyi ga dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das la rgyal po gzugs can snying pos lan gnyis su yang 'di

[\[98b.4\]]pa yang dag par 'dzin pa dang /_gsal bar bgyid pa'i rgyal rigs nas dmangs rigs kyi bar dag gi rigs rgyud rigs btsun pa dag 'byung bar 'gyur lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das de dag ni khyod kyi bka'i phrin las bstan pa thos nas stobs dang brtson pa mchog tu 
[\[98b.5\]]chen pos rgyal ba'i bstan pa legs par bstan pa gsal bar bgyid pa dang /_yongs su skyong ba dag tu 'gyur zhing /_gang zag de ltar yang dag par zhugs pa dag dang /_chos kyis gnas pa dag dang /_yon tan gyis phyug pa dag la yang mchod pa bgyid pa dang /_srung 
[\[98b.6\]]ba bgyid pa dang /_yongs su skyong bar bgyid par 'gyur lags so/_/khyim na gnas pa dang /_rab tu byung ba de dag kyang 'phrog par mi bgyid lags so/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das khyim na gnas pa gang gis gang zag chos kyis gnas pa dag gi longs spyod dang nye bar 
[\[98b.7\]]spyod pa phrogs nas bdag nyid kyis yongs su spyod par bgyid pa de'i rgud par 'gyur ba gang lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rgyal po chen po gal te ngas ma 'ongs pa'i dus na lung bong lta bu'i rgyal rigs nas dmangs rigs kyi bar dag chos kyis gnas pa/_
[\[99a\]]
[\[99a.1\]]yon tan gyis phyug pa dag la de ltar bsngos pa'i longs spyod dang nye bar spyod pa 'phrog cing bdag nyid kyis longs spyod par byed pa gang dag yin pa de dag gis log par sgrub pa 'byung ba de bshad na ni de dag kyang nga'i bka' bstan pa'i phrin las kyi skabs thos nas

[\[99a.5\]]zag de ltar yang dag par zhugs pa dag dang /_chos kyis gnas pa dag dang /_yon tan gyis phyug pa dag la yang mchod pa bgyid pa dang /_srung ba bgyid pa dang /_yongs su skyong bar bgyid par 'gyur lags so/_/khyim na gnas pa dang /_rab tu byung ba de dag kyang 
[\[99a.6\]]'phrog par mi bgyid lags so/_/chos kyis gnas pa de dag gi longs spyod dang nye bar spyod pa 'phrog par mi bgyid lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rgyal po chen po legs so legs so/_/khyod kyi spobs pa legs so/_/rgyal po chen po ma 
[\[99a.7\]]'ongs pa'i dus na gang dag rgyal rigs dang /_bram ze dang /_rje'u rigs dang /_dmangs rigs dag gis chos kyis gnas pa rnams la khyim gyi gzhi dang /_zhing gi gzhi dang /_kun dga'i ra ba'i gzhi dang /_skyed mos tshal dang /_bran dang /_bran mo dang /_mal cha dang /_
[\[99b\]]
[\[99b.1\]]stan dang /_na ba'i gsos sman dang /_yo byad dag de ltar bsngos pa dang /_rkang pa bzhi pa'i bar bsngos pa yang 'phrog par byed pa'am/_rku bar byed pa dag 'byung bar 'gyur te/_rgyal rigs nas dmangs rigs kyi bar de thams cad ni mthong ba'i chos la 'byung ba 'dod pa

[\[99b.6\]]longs spyod dag chud za bar 'gyur ba yin/_nad tshabs chen po dag gis kyang myur du thebs par 'gyur ba yin/_sman dang rim gro pa dang bral zhing sha dang khrag bskams nas sdug bsngal nyams su myong bar 'gyur ba yin/_rang gi bshang gci dag gi nang du 'chi ba'i dus byed par

[\[100a\]]
[\[100a.1\]]yongs su spyod par byed pa de 'chi ba'i dus byas nas sems can dmyal ba chen po mnar med par skye bar 'gyur ro/_/de der yang tshe bskal par lcags dang zangs bzhun 'thung bar 'gyur zhing /_lcags kyi thu lum za bar 'gyur la/_me'i gos dang /_
[\[100a.2\]]longs spyod dang /_nye bar spyod pa spyod par byed cing gnod pa mi bzad pa chen po rnam pa sna tshogs nyams su myong bar 'gyur ba yin no/_/tshe bskal pa zad nas kyang de nas shi 'phos nas 'brog dgon pa ro skam dag gi nang du lam du skyes pa'i [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]rnams dang skal pa mnyam 
[\[100a.3\]]par rkang pa dang /_lag pa dang /_mig ma tshang bar skye bar 'gyur ro/_/der yang phyogs bzhi nas langs pa'i rlung kun tu tsha ba dag gis kun tu gdung bar byed cing sa spu gri dang 'dra ba'i steng du dmyal bar byed/_yongs su dmyal bar byed de/_de der lo brgya stong mang por 
[\[100a.4\]]sdug bsngal de lta bu nyams su myong bar byed pa yin no/_/de gal te brgya la res 'ga' zhig de nas shi 'phos nas kyang rgya mtsho chen po'i nang du lus dpag tshad brgya pa'i sha nar nar por skye bar 'gyur ro/_/sngon gyi las kyi rgyus de'i dpag tshad khor yug gi chu zangs bzhun ma ltar 
[\[100a.5\]]dmar po 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/de der yang lo brgya stong mang por chu'i nang du sems can dmyal ba'i gnod pa myong bar byed pa yin no/_/de de nas shi 'phos nas kyang 'brog dgon pa chen por rdzus te skye ba sha nar nar po chen po ri dang mnyam pa 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/phyogs 
[\[100a.6\]]bzhi kun tu yang rlung kun tu tsha ba dag gis kun tu sreg par byed do/_/der yang phyogs bzhi nas [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dang bya dag lhags te yongs su za ba dag tu byed do/_/de brgya la res 'ga' dus ring zhig 'das nas de nas shi 'phos nas yang sems can dmyal ba chen po dag gi nang du skye 
[\[100a.7\]]bar 'gyur ro/_/de nas kyang shi 'phos nas yang dang yang du log par sgrub pa chen po de lta bu nyams su myong bar 'gyur te/_yongs su brtag pa nye bar bzung na/_de brgya lam na res 'ga' zhig mi rnams dang skal ba mnyam par skyes na yang sangs rgyas kyi zhing snyigs ma lnga dang 
[\[100b\]]
[\[100b.1\]]ldan pa dag tu mig ma tshang ba dang /_tshul du mi shis par skye bar 'gyur ro/_/rgyal po chen po gang chos kyis gnas pa dag la bsngos pa'i longs spyod dang nye bar spyod pa dang /_nor gyi yo byad 'phrog par byed pa de'i log par sgrub pa de ni de lta bu yin no/_/de nas rgyal po

[\[100b.3\]]nyid rnyed pa ni de lta ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rgyal po chen po da ltar dang ma 'ongs pa'i dus na rgyal rigs chos kyis gnas pa khyed cag gi lag tu gang zag yongs su gtad kyis srungs shig_/yongs su srungs shig_/rab tu byung ba'am/_

[\[186a.5\]]tshogs nas rol mo'i sgra dang /_gar dang glu sna tshogs pa'i bar gyis mchod pa byas nas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu smon lam btab bo/_/kha cig gis ni rkyen gyi theg par smon lam btab/_kha cig gis ni nyan thos kyi theg par smon 
[\[186a.6\]]lam btab ste/_thams cad kyang bsams pa ji lta ba bzhin du phyir mi ldog par gyur to/_/de'i tshe de dag cho ga bzhin du ting nge 'dzin dang /_bzod pa dang /_gzungs thob ste/_dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam pa sna tshogs bsgrubs nas rang gi zhing du dong ste/_byang chub sems

[\[195b.5\]]ldan chen po kye khyod ni/_/ci phyir ded la mngon par dga'/_/re ba ci 'dra ci la bsams/_/ci 'dod pa ni smra bar gyis/_/drang srong rnga bong gis smras pa/_/gza' dang rgyu skar skar ma rnams/_/mkha' la ci 'drar byung gyur pa'i/_/sngon gyi bskal pa bdag gis 
[\[195b.6\]]dran/_/nyin zhag du ni rgyu skar rnams/_/re re nas ni rgyu bar byed/_/tshe rabs kun tu rgyu skar gyi/_/yon tan las rnams rnam par shes/_/bskal pa bzang po 'di la ni/_/'di ni ci yang mi snang na/_/snying brtser gyis de khyod kyis ni/_/rgyu skar [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]kun 
[\[195b.7\]]zhog shig_/ji ltar 'jig rten 'dir gnas bzhin/_/de yi las dang yon tan gsungs/_/yon tan rgya mtshos shod cig ces/_/lha yi tshogs rnams kun gyis smras/_/drang srong rnga bong gis smras pa/_/re zhig dang por smin drug ces/_/rgyu skar 
[\[196a\]]
[\[196a.1\]]dkyil 'khor thams cad la/_/nang gi dang por gzhag par bya/_/ji ltar smin drug bzhag pa bzhin/_/'di la su zhig dad pa 'bul/_/lha yi bu ni nyi ma yang /_/skar ma drug po ston par byed/_/smin drug rgyu skar de dag ni/_/mkha' la rtag 
[\[196a.2\]]tu rgyu bar shog_/thams cad ldan pa'i 'khor dga' nas/_/me lha zhig ni sprul pa byas/_/gzi brjid chen po drang srong lha/_/me bzhin 'jug gis bus smras pa/_/bdag gi sring mo'i bu 'di dag_/gling bzhi par ni gnas par shog_/drang srong rnga bong slar 
[\[196a.3\]]smras pa/_/dang por smin drug de dag yin/_/rgyu skar chu yi rus can ni/_/gnyis pa yang ni yin par bya/_/skye dgu'i bdag gis 'di skad smras/_/bdag gis bu lnga dbul lags te/_/rus ni drang srong chen po chu/_/rab tu brtson ldan snar ma lags/_/
[\[196a.4\]]chu yi rus kyi rgyu skar ni/_/snar ma'i ming gis gnyis pa lags/_/zla ba'i bu pho gsum po ni/_/ming ni mgo zhes bgyi ba lags/_/ba ra dwa dza'i rus su ni/_/mgo zhes bgyi ba bzhag pa lags/_/rab tu mi bzad lag ces bya/_/lha drag rnams kyis 
[\[196a.5\]]bstan pa lags/_/ljang sngo'i bu yi bu rus su/_/de ni bzhi par bzhag pa lags/_/drang srong bcas pa'i lha tshogs kyis/_/nyi ma'i bu ni bstan lags te/_/nab so lags shing gnas 'jog rus/_/phur bu'i bu ni gsum lags te/_/lag gi bu 
[\[196a.6\]]yi rus lags shing /_/rgyal zhes bgyi bar de dag bshad/_/sa srung gi ni bu lnga po/_/brtul zhugs spyod pa mkhas pa rnams/_/mdza' bo'i bu yi rus lags te/_/de ltar de dag rgyu skar bdun/_/shar phyogs kha 'dzin rnams su bzhag_/de yi las 
[\[196a.7\]]dang [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]dang /_/sbyor ba kha 'dzin gzhung las bstan/_/ngas kyang yang ni khyed cag la/_/gling bzhi pa ni gnas byed cing /_/las ni ci 'dra sgrub byed pa/_/mdor ni myur du bshad par bya/_/phur bu yi ni bu lnga po/_/byang grol brtul zhugs 'chang ba dag_/
[\[196b\]]
[\[196b.1\]]ser skya'i bu yi rus su ni/_/lho yi phyogs su ngas bzhag go/_/dbyangs can gyi ni bu gnyis po/_/ming gis kyang ni gre yin te/_/drang srong gau ta ma rus su ni/_/lho yi phyogs su ngas bzhag go/_/lha mo yi ni bu gnyis po/_/ming gis kyang ni dbo yin te/_/
[\[196b.2\]]kau Di n+ya yi rus su ni/_/lho phyogs kha 'dzin gsum pa bzhag_/sdig pa yi ni bu bstan pa/_/drang srong kA tA'i bu yi rus/_/lhar bcas drang srong rnams kyis der/_/me bzhi zhes ni bya bar bzhag_/dri za'i bu ni nag pa ste/_/kA tA'i bu yi rus yin no/_/
[\[196b.3\]]sa ri de ni ma yi bu/_/kA tA'i bu chen rus yin no/_/'dir ni sa ga dpal gyi bu/_/kA tA'i bu rgan rus yin te/_/drang srong rnams kyis rgyu skar bdun/_/lho phyogs kha 'dzin rnams su bzhag_/mdza' bo'i bu yi bu bzhi po/_/drag po'i bu yi rus yin te/_/ming 
[\[196b.4\]]gis lha mtshams zhes bya ba/_/nub phyogs su ni bzhag pa yin/_/dbang po yi ni bu gsum ste/_/zla ba kA tA'i bu yi rus/_/'jam pa'i las ni thams cad la/_/gnyis pa snron ni bzhag pa yin/_/sa yi lha mo'i bu gsum ste/_/snrubs ni lag pa'i 
[\[196b.5\]]bu yi rus/_/rgyal ba'i lha mo'i bu bzhi ste/_/kA tA'i bu yi rus yin no/_/thams cad lha zhes bya ba bzhi/_/maud gal bu yi rus yin no/_/snrubs dang chu stod chu smad gnyis/_/rgyu skar gsum po bzhag pa yin/_/sred med bu yi bu gsum po/_/rgyu skar 
[\[196b.6\]]rnams ni byi bzhin 'khod/_/khyab 'jug gi ni bu gsum ste/_/kA tA'i bu pho dpal gyi rus/_/de ltar de dag rgyu skar bdun/_/nub phyogs kyi ni kha 'dzin yin/_/nor lha'i bu ni mon gru ste/_/drang srong rna ba'i rus yin no/_/chu lha'i bu ni gcig pu ste/_/deb 
[\[196b.7\]]rtse'i bu yi rus yin zhing /_/sman ma sprul gyi lha mo yi/_/rgyu skar mon gre gnas pa yin/_/sdig pa yi ni bu gnyis te/_/khrums stod ces ni bya bar bshad/_/chu yi rna ba'i rus su ni/_/gsum pa 'di ni bzhag pa yin/_/lha yi drang srong chu lha'i bu/_/
[\[197a\]]
[\[197a.1\]]khrums smad ces ni bya bar bshad/_/bra nye drang srong rus yin par/_/bzhi pa 'di ni bzhag pa yin/_/lha yi drang srong rgyal gyi bu/_/nam gru zhes bya de bstan te/_/drang srong dbang po'i rus su ni/_/lnga pa 'di ni bzhag pa yin/_/dri za'i rgyal po'i 
[\[197a.2\]]bu gsum ste/_/lnga len bu yi rus yin zhing /_/rgyu skar tha skar zhes bya ba/_/'jig rten mkhas pa rnams kyis bzhag_/drag po'i bu ni gsum yin te/_/bra nye ngan spong rus yin no/_/rgyu skar bdun po de dag ni/_/byang phyogs kha 'dzin rnams su bzhag_/
[\[197a.3\]]rgyu skar nyi shu rtsa brgyad po/_/kha 'dzin bzhir ni gzhung las bshad/_/gtor ma las dang yon tan rnams/_/mdor na nga yis bshad par bya/_/thams cad rab tu thal sbyar 'khod/_/lha dang shed las skyes rnams kyis/_/drang srong rnga bong la ni smras/_/mi dge 
[\[197a.4\]]sngon du 'gro ba'i las/_/gang byas khyod ni mi sdug skyes/_/gang du thams cad myur zad 'gyur/_/'di ni lha dang mi rnams dang /_/drang srong dag ni zhi ba rnams/_/thams cad gzugs ni bzang por gyur/_/rigs dang snyan ldan de dag kun/_/drang srong rnga bong 
[\[197a.5\]]de la ni/_/brgya byin lhan cig 'khod nas su/_/'khor kun rangs te thal sbyar gyur/_/bsam pa gcig tu smras pa ni/_/nam mkha' 'di la rgyu skar ni/_/gang zhig dang por rgyu bar byed/_/kha zas su ni ci za shod/_/mi rnams kyi ni las ci sgrub/_/yun ni ji srid 
[\[197a.6\]]rgyu bar byed/_/drang srong de dag thams cad la/_/rnga bong gis ni ji skad smras/_/ji ltar nga yis brjod pa bzhin/_/'dir ni khyed kyis dad pa phul/_/rgyu skar 'di la ngas mchog phyin/_/de ni khyod la brjod par bya/_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]shes pa nga bdag bzhin/_/gling 
[\[197a.7\]]bzhi'i sems can phan pa'i phyir/_/lo skor yang ni shes par bya/_/dang por nyi ma gang yin pa/_/ngas byas pa ni nga yi mig_/bu drug g.yo can sems rangs pa/_/de ni nga yis dang por brjod/_/de yi phyir na smin drug ni/_/rgyu skar tshogs ni thams cad du/_/
[\[197b\]]
[\[197b.1\]]nang gi gtso bor 'gyur bar shog_/zla ba nya dang nyi ma yi/_/nyin mtshan de dag mnyam par gnas/_/mtshan mo gzhan ni mun khyab dang /_/chu lha yang ni gnas par 'gyur/_/'gro ba bzod pa bgrang ba dang /_/bra nye gnas bzhin 'di ltar bshad/_/mon 
[\[197b.2\]]sran bcu drug brgyar bsgyur te/_/skyes bu dang ni brgyad pa gcig_/brgyad pa phrag ni bcu drug la/_/cha ni gcig tu 'gyur ba yin/_/cha ni drug cu tham pa la/_/yud tsam cig ni de yin no/_/de tsam yud tsam sum cu la/_/'dir ni 'jig rten gnas pa 
[\[197b.3\]]yin/_/ri rab g.yas nas bskor ba'i tshe/_/yud tsam sum cus 'gro bar byed/_/'dir ni mon sran tshod ma za/_/bya ba nyung ngu bsgrub par gyis/_/rgyal po nad pas 'gro ba kun/_/zhag bcu tham pa thar bar gyis/_/rtag tu dad pas mtshon chas bsrungs/_/
[\[197b.4\]]mchog sbyin khyod kyis bu skye gang /_/tshor ba dbang po thul ba dang /_/chos kyi blo gros nab so la/_/mngal du nying mtshams sbyor bar 'gyur/_/'dir ni gre la bya ba mang /_/chu smad bra nye gsum pa yin/_/me bzhi snar ma gro bzhin ni/_/nor rgud byed par 'gyur 
[\[197b.5\]]ba yin/_/snrubs dang skag dang nam gru ni/_/lam dag gi ni rgyu skar yin/_/khrums stod dang ni snron dang ni/_/rgyal dang yang ni khrums smad ni/_/phun sum tshogs par byed pa yin/_/mchu dang chu stod tha skar dang /_/mon gru dang ni smin drug dang /_/dbo zhes 
[\[197b.6\]]bya ba dag gis ni/_/bde ba sgrub par byed pa yin/_/'dir ni mgo dang nag pa dang /_/mon gru dang ni mon gre dang /_/sa ri lag ni rnam drug gis/_/grogs po dang ni grogs mchog 'gyur/_/lha yi dbang pos 'di skad smras/_/snron gyi tshe na tshes gnyis la/_/
[\[197b.7\]]yud tsam sum cur mi zad par/_/bya phyir mkha' la khyod gnas kyis/_/khyod kyis kyang ni la phug dang /_/bu ram zas la gnas par gyis/_/nad pa zhag ni bcu gsum khrol/_/da ltar skye dgu skye ba med/_/shin tu tshul 'chal snying rje spangs/_/mchod sbyin 
[\[198a\]]
[\[198a.1\]]dang ni skye ba dang /_/snron la skye tshe rtag tu ni/_/mngal du mtshams sbyor bsrung bar gyis/_/nag pa dang ni mon gru dang /_/mgo la grags pa mang du byed/_/byi bzhin dang ni gre dang dbo/_/las dag gi ni rgyu skar yin/_/khrums stod dang ni khrums smad 
[\[198a.2\]]ni/_/rgud byed chu dang chu stod dang /_/tha skar phun sum tshogs par byed/_/snrubs dang skag dang nam gru dang /_/mon gre me bzhi snar ma ni/_/bde ba sgrub par byed pa yin/_/bra nye dang ni gre dang ni/_/chu smad mdza' bo'i mchog tu 'gyur/_/khrums stod dang ni sa ri dang /_/
[\[198a.3\]]mon gru dang ni nab so dang /_/sa ga dang ni lag kyang ni/_/'dir ni rnam pa drug tu 'gyur/_/lha yi drang srong des gsob kyis/_/snrubs bshad pa na tshes gsum la/_/yud tsam sum cur nam mkha' la/_/bya ba che chung khyod gnas kyis/_/zas ni 'bras bu la phug dang /_/
[\[198a.4\]]bdug pa dang ni me tog ste/_/zhag ni sum cur bdag nyid kyis/_/nad pa yang ni thar bar byed/_/gang zhig spyod par byed pas ni/_/skye dgu mi skye nor dang 'bru/_/skal ldan skyes bu'i rnam pa dang /_/sa la las ni byed par 'gyur/_/mgo la mngal du mtshams sbyor 
[\[198a.5\]]ba/_/rta dang yang ni rnga mo dang /_/glang po che dang khyu mchog dang /_/dred dang bcas pa'i rnam par 'gyur/_/chu stod dang ni gre dang ni/_/bra nye dang ni gsum pa ste/_/'jig pa'i rgyur 'gyur skag dang snrubs/_/de la 'phel bar byed par 'gyur/_/nam gru bgrang dang mnyam 'gyur 
[\[198a.6\]]te/_/mon gru las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/khrums stod ces ni bya ba yang /_/de la rgud par byed par 'gyur/_/lha mtshams nab so gsum pa yin/_/lag ni de la rgud byed 'gyur/_/sa ga dang ni mon gre yang /_/gsum pa zhes ni bya ba yin/_/tha skar de la sgrub par 
[\[198a.7\]]'gyur/_/chu stod zla ba gnyis pa ste/_/snron dang rgyal zhes bya ba yang /_/de la bde bar byed par 'gyur/_/khrums smad ces ni bya ba ni/_/grogs dang grogs po mchog tu 'gyur/_/drug po 'di dag lag dang ni/_/gro bzhin snar ma gsum pa yin/_/smin drug 
[\[198b\]]
[\[198b.1\]]ces ni bya ba yang /_/byi bzhin dang ni dbo yin no/_/'dir ni khron pa sa rir gyur/_/me lha yin te sa gas bshad/_/gling bzhir tshes ni [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]lnga la/_/khyod spyod yud tsam bzhi bcu lngas/_/'gro bar byed cing bya ba ni/_/chen po mi zad pa yis gnas/_/zhag ni [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]lnga 
[\[198b.2\]]tham par yang /_/nad pa yang ni thar bar gyis/_/su zhig kyang ni khyod kyi bur/_/dag par rjes su skye 'gyur ba/_/gzi brjid dang ni dpal dang ni/_/longs spyod dga' ba 'bar bar 'gyur/_/bud med brnab sems shin tu srungs/_/de skye'i rgyu skar lag yin 
[\[198b.3\]]no/_/de'i las rgyu skar mon gre yin/_/mchu dang tha skar chu stod rgyu/_/smin drug ces ni bya ba la/_/de ni 'chi bar 'gyur ba yin/_/byi bzhin dang ni dbo yang ni/_/'dir ni rgud par byed pa yin/_/khrums stod dang ni snron dang ni/_/nab so yang ni gsum pa ste/_/
[\[198b.4\]]lha mtshams zhes ni bya bar yang /_/de ni phun sum tshogs byed yin/_/nab so khrums stod snrubs dang ni/_/nam gru skag ni bde byed yin/_/gre dang yang ni chu smad dang /_/gsum pa bra nye sgrub pa yin/_/mdza' bo dang ni mdza' mchog tu/_/de dag der ni 'gyur ba 
[\[198b.5\]]yin/_/nag pa snar ma mon gru bzhin/_/mgo gnyis me bzhi gro bzhin yang /_/'dir ni de bdun lho phyogs kyi/_/kha 'dzin phrugs zhes bya ba yin//_//'phags pa shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i sde nyi ma'i snying po zhes bya ba'i mdo/_bam po bcu 
[\[198b.6\]]pa/_mdza' bshes kyi ni lha mo yis/_/yar gyi ngo yi tshes gcig la/_/'gro bar byed pa'i [{]go rims kyang [,]go rim yang [}]/_/lha mtshams la ni smras pa yin/_/bra nye tshes ni [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]lnga yi/_/nyin par yang ni yud tsam yin/_/mtshan mo yang ni de tsam ste/_/nyin mtshan gcig la 
[\[198b.7\]]bra nye yin/_/chu yi lha mo de yang ni/_/nyin dang mtshan du mnyam pa ste/_/yud tsam sum cu tham pa yin/_/de yi longs spyod thang cig shog_/rgyal po legs brtan snying rje spangs/_/'thun par yul 'khor skyong ba bzhin/_/de bzhin du ni mi zad pa'i/_/bra 
[\[199a\]]
[\[199a.1\]]nye yang ni 'gro bar shog_/'dzam gling 'di la ston par shog_/de bzhin khyed cag thams cad kyis/_/de la dmod pa bor bar gyis/_/de bzhin 'gro la bstan par gyis/_/kun gyis smras pa/_de bzhin rtag tu gyur par shog_/mi zad pa yi bya 
[\[199a.2\]]ba gyis/_/khyod kyis spyod pas gang skye ba/_/mi yi thu bo mi bzad 'gyur/_/de yi mngal 'jug chu stod yin/_/bra nye la ni skyes pa'i mi/_/'chi ba'i tshe na lha mtshams dang /_/khrums stod ces ni bya ba yin/_/'di la mkhas pas brtag bya ste/_/sa ri 
[\[199a.3\]]mon gru ma yin gzhi/_/dmag 'gro rtsod pa'i gnas dag gis/_/bya spang spu gri'i las mi bya/_/snar ma me bzhi byi bzhin ni/_/rgud byed dgra rnams khyod kyis gzhom/_/khyod kyis las ni che chung kun/_/gre yi tshe na bya bar gyis/_/chu smad dang ni dbo yang ni/_/
[\[199a.4\]]phun sum tshogs pa byed par byed/_/sman rnams brten bya sbyin dang dul/_/gos dang rgyan gyi bya ba'ang brjod/_/mgo dang nag pa zhes bya dang /_/gro bzhin mchu ni bde bar byed/_/sa ga nab so mon gre ste/_/de dag lam 'jug sgrub pa yin/_/skag dang snrubs zhes bya ba 
[\[199a.5\]]dang /_/tha skar dang ni mchu dang gre/_/grogs po mchog tu 'gyur ba yin/_/bsgrub tu mi rung las kyang gyis/_/'di tshe khri bzhon gos dang mal/_/mdza' bo rnams la'ang brtsam par bya/_/nyi ma'i bu ni mig dmar te/_/drang srong chen po der dpang btsugs/_/bcu 
[\[199a.6\]]gnyis khyim dus gnas de nas/_/der yod las 'thun grib gom drug_/der ni dus kyang mnyam pa yin/_/nyin dang mtshan kyang mtshungs pa yin/_/de yod tha skar mtshan mo gang /_/lha mtshams nyin par gnas pa na/_/khyod kyis nga ni spang bar gyis/_/mkha' 
[\[199a.7\]]la spyod cing khyod kyis ston/_/gling bzhi pa ni mgu bya'i phyir/_/'di ni ji ltar lhags pa bzhin/_/ngas 'dir gang zhig yang dag brjod/_/lha yi drang srong kun dga' gyur/_/de bzhin khyod ni kun du song /_/smin drug de bzhin me yi lha/_/bdag nyid gling bzhi der 
[\[199b\]]
[\[199b.1\]]gnas shing /_/khyod kyis me bzhin myur bya gyis/_/yud tsam sum cur tshal du song /_/der gang mi yi zas 'byung ba/_/dam pa'i grags dang longs spyod kyang /_/zla nya khyod 'dir yang legs rdzogs/_/de ltar nga yis thob pa yin/_/zhag bzhi tham par 
[\[199b.2\]]nad pa ni/_/myur ba kho nar thar bar gyis/_/mngal du 'jug pa me bzhi bsrungs/_/chu stod 'chi bar byed pa yin/_/skag khrums stod smad nab so dang /_/mon gre 'phel bar byed pa yin/_/khyod kyis bya ba 'di spongs shig_/dmag dang rtsod pa'i gnas 'gro 
[\[199b.3\]]dang /_/spu gri yi ni las rnams so/_/gro bzhin dang ni nag pa dang /_/mgo ni rgud par byed pa yin/_/lag dang dbo ni 'dir 'joms byed/_/byed pa'i bya ba 'di gyis shig_/che dang chung ba gang ci yang /_/snar mas phun sum tshogs par byed/_/byi bzhin 
[\[199b.4\]]dang ni nag par bcas/_/der ni bya ba gang rtsi dang /_/gom pa sbyin pa gos dang mal/_/der ni rgyan gyis brgyan par bya/_/lag ni bde bar byed pa ste/_/mon gru dang ni sa ri yang /_/gsum po dag ni de dag yin/_/lam du 'jug pa'ang de dag yin/_/rgyal 
[\[199b.5\]]dang lha mtshams khrums stod ni/_/las dag sgrub par byed pa yin/_/mchu snrubs skag dang khrums smad dang /_/de dang de rnams drag byed grogs/_/skal bzang bag ma gtong dang bzhon/_/mal yang der ni de bya ste/_/khyim dang dus zhes bya ba dang /_/gza' 
[\[199b.6\]]rnams bdag gis brjod pa yin/_/de ltar bdag gis dus kun brjod/_/khyod ci drang srong lha mi dga'/_/bdag gis byed de bshad pa bzhin/_/lha dbang drang srong 'khor dang bcas/_/mi yis thal sbyar 'di skad smras/_/'byung po kun la phan pa khyod/_/'gro 
[\[199b.7\]]ba la ni skyob pa khyod/_/de ltar shes mnyam ji bzhin khyod/_/yang smras pa/_rnga bong can gyi drang srong ste/_/snar ma chu yi rigs kyang yin/_/chu yi lha mo khyod yin na/_/gsum par yang ni 'gro bar gyis/_/byed pa chung dang 'bring yin zhing /_/bzhi bcu rtsa lnga tham 
[\[200a\]]
[\[200a.1\]]pa yin/_/yud tsam du ni khyod kyis 'gro/_/dug dang sha ni zas yin no/_/khyod la spyod par byed pa yis/_/gang dag mi rnams skye 'gyur ba/_/de dag phyug cing chos dang ldan/_/zhag lnga nad pa myur du gsos/_/de 'dir khyod kyis bstan par gyis/_/
[\[200a.2\]]byi bzhin nam grus mngal du 'jug_/'chi ba snrubs dang mchu bzhin te/_/rgyal dang lha mtshams khrums stod 'phel/_/dmag 'gro bag ma gtong ba yi/_/gnas nas 'gro bar byed pa dang /_/nyo tshong dang ni bzo byed pa/_/de 'dra ba yi bya ba spongs/_/sa ri mon 
[\[200a.3\]]gru de dag 'dir/_/rgud byed dgra 'dir gzhom par bya/_/me bzhi las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/che chung thams cad 'dir gyis shig_/mgo dang nag pa gro bzhin te/_/de dag phun sum tshogs par byed/_/rtsi byed na yang de dag sten/_/der byin gos rgyan sna tshogs sgrogs/_/
[\[200a.4\]]nab so dang ni sa ga dang /_/mon gre bde bar byed pa ste/_/de dag la ni lam du 'jug_/snron ni sgrub pa byed pa yin/_/skag ces bya ba khrums smad ni/_/drag po'i las dag byed pa yin/_/chu stod chu smad tha skar dang /_/bra nye khrums stod khrums smad mdza'/_/
[\[200a.5\]]dbos ni skal ba bzang po sbyin/_/brtson pa dang ni bzhon pa dang /_/mal dang khri ni bsham pa yang /_/'di la bya ba de 'jug go/_/yang smras pa/_'gro ba bzhi dang ldan par ni/_/mgo la khyod ni gnas par 'gyur/_/mi yi las bya myur du sgrubs/_/yud tsam 
[\[200a.6\]]sum cur 'gro bar gyis/_/zhag brgyad nad pa myur du gsos/_/khyod zas tshon sha zla ba'i lha/_/der ni skyes dgu gang dag skye/_/dpa' zhing 'byor bar 'gyur ba yin/_/khyab 'jug bden 'di shin tu bsrungs/_/de byed ji bzhin nga yis bshad/_/gro bzhin tha skar 
[\[200a.7\]]mngal du 'jug_/mchu gnyis gre dbo 'chi bar byed/_/snron gyi tshe ni bya ba 'phel/_/skag dang gzhan bcas thob par byed/_/lam ring rtsod dang gnas nas 'gro/_/bya ba de 'dra spang bar gyis/_/nab so rgud par byed pa ste/_/mon gre sa ga bzhin du shes/_/gsang 
[\[200b\]]
[\[200b.1\]]ba'i gnas bzhin brtsam par gyis/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]g.yo dmangs kyis 'joms par byed/_/nag pa las kyi rgyu skar te/_/'di la che chung thams cad rtsom/_/lag ni phun sum tshogs par byed/_/mon gru sa ri gzhan thob byed/_/'dir ni sman dang sbyin 'dul 
[\[200b.2\]]bzhon/_/rgyan dang gos kyang thob par byed/_/rgyal dang lha mtshams khrums stod bde/_/de dag lam du shin tu 'phrod/_/snrubs dang nam gru mchu sgrub byed/_/'di la gtum po'i las kyang bya/_/me bzhi dgra zin bra nye dang /_/snar ma dbyer med chu gnyis 
[\[200b.3\]]bzhin/_/skal bzang bag ma gtong dang bzhon/_/mal kun 'dir ni byed du chug_/lag lha drag mos de tshe smras/_/tshes lnga la ni khyod gnas te/_/yud tsam bco lnga khyod 'gro byed/_/khyod kyi zas ni mar sar te/_/drag cing mi bzad sdang byed sgrub/_/nad 
[\[200b.4\]]pa yang ni zhag bcu yin/_/khyod la spyad pas mi gang skye/_/srid pa gsum du mi zad 'gyur/_/nor tshol byed cing chom pos 'chi/_/khyod kyi sbyor bas de yin no/_/mon gru snrubs la mngal du 'jug_/chu dang nam grus 'phel bar 'gyur/_/dbo yi tshe na 'chi 
[\[200b.5\]]bar byed/_/chu gnyis bra nye ji bzhin no/_/skye dang 'jig dang 'phel ba yi/_/byed pa de ni spong bar gyis/_/lha mtshams dang ni khrums stod der/_/byed pa bstan nas rgud par byed/_/sa ri las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/byed pa chung ngu thams cad 'jug_/sa ga dang ni 
[\[200b.6\]]mon gre dang /_/nab so phun sum tshogs par byed/_/khrums smad par ni bde byed yin/_/snron dang skag ni lam na mnyam/_/gre ni sgrub par byed pa ste/_/chu stod tha skar gsum pa yin/_/me bzhi'i bar gzhan grogs mchog ste/_/smin drug nag pa gro bzhin 
[\[200b.7\]]dang /_/byi bzhin bag ma gtong dang bzhon/_/mal dang skal ba bzang por sgrub/_/nyi ma'i lha mo nab sos smras/_/tshes drug la yang gnas par 'gyur/_/sbrul sha khyod kyi zas su shog_/las kyang drag por bya bar gyis/_/yud tsam bzhi bcu rtsa lngar 'gro/_/
[\[201a\]]
[\[201a.1\]]'dir ni nad pa zhag brgyad yin/_/khyod spyad su yang mi nang skye/_/zhing pa longs spyod ldan par 'gyur/_/rta dang glang chen zad par 'gro/_/nab sos mngal du 'jug par 'gyur/_/'di ni mon gre'i zas yin te/_/smin drug la ni 'chi bar byed/_/
[\[201a.2\]]khrums smad rgud par byed pa yin/_/skag snron las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/sa ga brtul zhugs bstan pa yin/_/rgyal yang phun sum tshogs par byed/_/lag dang lha mtshams zhes bya dang /_/khrums stod kyang ni bde bar byed/_/mchu dang nam gru bzhin du ste/_/
[\[201a.3\]]de dag lam du zhugs na bzang /_/bra nye chu smad gre dang dbo/_/sgrub byed nag pa gro bzhin dang /_/sa ri snar ma mgo mon gru/_/mi yi nang khyod grogs mchog 'gyur/_/rgyal 'dir lha mo phur bu khyod/_/tshes bdun gnas khyod yud sum cu/_/'gro byed bu ram sbrang rtsi 
[\[201a.4\]]zas/_/chos ldan byed chud myur du sgrub/_/nad kyang zhag lnga kho na ste/_/khyod spyad su yang mi nang skye/_/tshul dga' chos smras mdun 'don blon/_/zhing ring bag gtong de dag 'jig_/khrums stod dang ni lha mtshams la/_/mngal du 'jug par byed pa ste/_/
[\[201a.5\]]dbo dang yang ni tha skar dang /_/chu smad la ni 'phel bar byed/_/snar ma nag pa gro bzhin 'chi/_/gsum po de dag shes par gyis/_/mchu snrubs nam gru de dag gsum/_/rgud byed mkhas pas shes par gyis/_/lha mtshams byed pa thams cad la/_/las 
[\[201a.6\]]dag gi ni rgyu skar te/_/mkhas pas shes pa kho nar gyis/_/snron ni phun sum tshogs par byed/_/khrums smad skag gis thob byed gre/_/chu stod tha skar gsum po bde/_/me bzhi sgrub min drug dbyer med/_/de bzhin mkhas pas gsum shes gyis/_/sa ri 
[\[201a.7\]]grogs dang grogs mchog ste/_/mgo dang lag dang mon gru bzhin/_/sa ga dang ni mon gre yang /_/don ni 'bras bur bcas pa yin/_/sbrul gyi lha mo skag 'dir smra/_/tshes brgyad la ni khyod gnas kyis/_/yud tsam bco lngas khyod 'gro byed/_/god 
[\[201b\]]
[\[201b.1\]]pa'i sha ni zas yin no/_/'gro ba'i nang du gshin rje yi/_/byed pa mi zad khyod gnas kyis/_/nyin der khyod spyod su mir skye/_/khro zhing sdig pa can du 'gyur/_/shin tu mi sdug tshul khrims nyams/_/chos gos gding ba zad 'gyur zhing /_/khrums smad ces 
[\[201b.2\]]bya rab mchog la/_/mngal du 'jug pa ston par byed/_/me bzhi smin drug 'chi bar byed/_/mgo snrubs gre ni ro myangs pas/_/rgud byed chu stod tha skar dang /_/snron ni las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/snrubs dang nam gru mchu zhes pa/_/gsum po phun sum tshogs par 
[\[201b.3\]]byed/_/bra nye bde byed gro dbo dang /_/gro bzhin mchu gnyis khyod thos sgrub/_/nag pa snar ma mchu gnyis dang /_/mon gre lha mtshams sa ga dang /_/lag dang nab so de dag grogs/_/khrums stod kyang ni de bzhin no/_/gshin rje'i [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]lha mos smras/_/
[\[201b.4\]]tshes dgu la ni khyod gnas shing /_/yud tsam sum cur nyin mtshan du/_/til gyi sbyar thug zas yin no/_/sems can byed pa chung 'bring sgrubs/_/zhag brgyad tham par nad pa sos/_/gang ma'i bra nye su mir skye/_/bsod nams byed par bshad pa yin/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]
[\[201b.5\]]srog shin tu yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]bsrungs/_/snrubs ni las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/'dir yang nam grus mngal du 'jug_/sa ga la yang 'chi bar byed/_/lag ces bya ba kho na dang /_/mon gre yang ni gsum pa yin/_/snar ma dang ni nag pa dang /_/gsum pa gro bzhin 'dir 
[\[201b.6\]]dga' byed/_/dbo dang bra nye rgud byed yin/_/chu smad phun sum tshogs par byed/_/tha skar dang ni gre dang ni/_/mchu gnyis 'phel bar byed pa yin/_/gsum po de dag shes par gyis/_/me bzhi dang ni byi bzhin dang /_/smin drug kyang ni bde bar byed/_/sa ri de la 
[\[201b.7\]]sgrub pa yin/_/mgo gnyis dang ni mon gru bcas/_/de gsum rje grogs shes par gyis/_/snron rgyal lha mtshams chu stod dang /_/nab so lhag par zla ba'ang yin/_/skal ldan lha mo gre yis smras/_/tshes bcu la ni khyod gnas shig_/bil ba mar sar zas yin 
[\[202a\]]
[\[202a.1\]]te/_/yud tsam sum cur khyod kyis 'gro/_/'gro 'dir bya ba chung ngu sgrubs/_/zhag bdun nad pa khyod spyod pas/_/su zhig mir skye dri dga' 'dod/_/gos dang rgyan dag 'dod pa yin/_/bye brag phyed rnams su srung ba/_/tha skar la ni mngal 'jug 
[\[202a.2\]]'gyur/_/nab sos 'chi mgo zin ro myang /_/'phel byed smin drug rgud par byed/_/me bzhi'ang mkhas pas mngon shes gyis/_/gre zhes bya dang bra nye dang /_/chu smad ces ni bya ba gsum/_/phun sum tshogs byed shes par gyis/_/'di ni sa ga mon gre yang /_/
[\[202a.3\]]sgrub yin lag la'ang bde ba ste/_/snar ma nag pa gro bzhin nyin/_/mkhas pas grogs gnyis shes par gyis/_/rnam pa drug tu 'gyur ba ni/_/skag dang nam gru rgyal dag dang /_/snrubs dang snron zhes bya ba dang /_/khrums gnyis gang yin 'dir yin 
[\[202a.4\]]no/_/lhag par yang ni de bzhin du/_/thams cad kyang ni bgrang bar gyis/_/mkhas pa blo gros ldan pa yis/_/rang gi thams cad shes par gyis/_/gre dbo nyi ma'i lha mos smras/_/tshes ni bcu gcig khyod gnas kyis/_/yud tsam bzhi bcu rtsa lnga yis/_/
[\[202a.5\]]khyod ni rnam par grol bar gyis/_/mgo yis las ni chung ngus kyang /_/sa bon thob cing bskyed bya ste/_/zhag bcur nad pa zhi bar gyis/_/khyod spyad skye dgu gang 'byung ba/_/blun zhing bsnyen gnas gnas pa dang /_/sbyin bral 'gro la sdang mi sdug_/dug gis 
[\[202a.6\]]'chi bar 'gyur ba ste/_/de yi mngal 'jug bra nye shog_/nam gru mon gre 'phel bar byed/_/lag ces bya dang sa ga dang /_/snron zhes bya ba gsum pa ste/_/de dag 'chi bar byed pa yin/_/khrums smad dang ni rgyal yang 'dir/_/rgud byed yin par shes par gyis/_/
[\[202a.7\]]snar ma dang ni chu smad kyang /_/de'i las rgyu skar shes par gyis/_/me bzhi'ang phun sum tshogs byed cing /_/byi bzhin dang ni smin drug kyang /_/bde bar byed pa'i gnas yin te/_/mon gru dang ni mgo sgrub byed/_/nab so khrums stod lha mtshams 'dir/_/mkhas pa 
[\[202b\]]
[\[202b.1\]]bzhin du'ang shes par gyis/_/grogs dang grogs mchog tha skar te/_/mchu dang nam gru skag dang bcas/_/snrubs dang khrums stod ces bya ba/_/drug po de dag nam mkha' la/_/nyi ma'i lha mo lhan cig par/_/mkhas pa yis ni shes par gyis/_/bcu 
[\[202b.2\]]gnyis khru myur rgyal byed mkha'i/_/dpal las bya bar myur mi bzad/_/yud tsam sum cur 'gro bar gyis/_/sems can rnams la phan par byin/_/e ma khyod zas lham mchu gsher/_/zhag lnga nad pa myur du gsos/_/der gnas skye dgu gang skye ba/_/
[\[202b.3\]]dbang phyug gtso bo nor ldan yin/_/chom rkun rnams ni 'dul byed mchog_/sbyin dang dul bar byed pa yin/_/mchog sbyin rnam pa sna tshogs kyi/_/mchod sbyin byed par 'gyur ba yin/_/byang chub kyi ni yan lag bdun/_/go 'phang srung bar byed pas na/_/
[\[202b.4\]]de yi grags pa mngal 'jug kyang /_/skag ces bya ba kho na yin/_/lha mtshams 'chi bar byed pa ste/_/de yi 'phel byed nab so yin/_/khrums stod ces ni bya ba yang /_/las kyi rgyu skar dbyer med pa'o/_/sa ri mon gru 'dir rgud byed/_/'dir ni 
[\[202b.5\]]mgo yang gsum pa yin/_/phun sum tshogs byed 'dir gro bzhin/_/sa ri snar ma'ang gsum pa yin/_/mon gre bde bar byed pa ste/_/'dir ni sa ga lag gsum pa/_/snron dang rgyal yang sgrub pa ste/_/'dir ni khrums smad gsum pa'ang yin/_/grogs dang 
[\[202b.6\]]grogs mchog 'di drug ste/_/chu stod mchu dang tha skar dang /_/chu smad ces ni bya ba dang /_/gre dang bra nye 'chi bar byed/_/dga' byed lha mo nag pas smras/_/khyod mkha' myur song yud sum cur/_/mi bzad las byed myur gnas shig_/yan lag rmas pa'ang khyod 
[\[202b.7\]]kyis bza'/_/zhag drug nad pa myur du gsos/_/khyod la gnas pas skye dgu skye/_/glu dang rol mo'i sgra mkhas 'gyur/_/sbyin dga' 'dod spyod mkhas la sdug_/snar ma de yi mngal 'jug yin/_/tha skar zhes ni bya ba dang /_/mchu ni 'chi bar byed pa ste/_/chu stod ces 
[\[203a\]]
[\[203a.1\]]bya gsum pa'ang yin/_/khrums smad 'phel bar byed pa ste/_/snron dang rgyal ni gsum pa'ang yin/_/de yi las kyi rgyu skar ni/_/gro bzhin yin gyi bgrang bar gyis/_/'dir ni mon gre rgud par byed/_/sa ga 'dir lag gsum pa'ang yin/_/mgo

[\[203a.3\]]drug chu smad smin drug dang /_/bra nye dang ni dbo dang yang /_/sa bon gre dang sa ri ste/_/pha mas 'dir smras gling bzhi bar/_/mkha' la khyod song tshes bcu bzhir/_/khyod spyod 'gro ba'i gling bzhi bar/_/lnga yud drag byos khyod song shig_/me tog nas shin zas yin te/_/

[\[203a.5\]]dang /_/gsum pa khrums stod sgrub pa yin/_/'dir bde nag pa mgo mon gru/_/rnam pa drug ni mdza' bo yin/_/nam gru snron mdza' tha skar dang /_/mchu snrubs de dag mchog tu mdza'/_/byi bzhin tshangs pa'i lha mos smras/_/tshes drug mkha' la khyod yud 
[\[203a.6\]]song /_/mi bzad bya ba 'byung bya'i phyir/_/mar sar yang ni khyod zo shig_/khyod la sems can su gnas pa/_/dpa' zhing sran thub 'byor rgyal gtso/_/yul 'khor grong 'joms dgra thul ba/_/dbo la de ni mngal du 'jug_/de la 'phel byed 'chi ba med/_/

[\[203b.1\]]grogs skyong rnams kyis mchod pa yin/_/'dir de me bzhis mngal 'jug yin/_/rgyal zhes bya dang lha mtshams dang /_/khrums stod la ni 'phel bar byed/_/mchu snrubs gro bzhin 'chi bar byed/_/snar ma las kyi rgyu skar yin/_/sa ri mon gre lag rgud

[\[203b.4\]]'dir de'i mngal 'jug nag pa yin/_/gre dang bra nye 'chi bar byed/_/chu stod gsum par bgrang bar gyis/_/snron skag nam gru 'phel bar byed/_/sa ga nab so khrums stod rgud/_/sa ri dang ni mon gre dang /_/lag ni phun sum tshogs par byed/_/las kun 
[\[203b.5\]]gnyen gyis shin tu bsrungs/_/de yi mngal 'jug skag yin no/_/mon gre zhes ni bya ba dang /_/lag ni 'chi bar byed pa ste/_/me bzhi dang ni mon gre dang /_/snar ma 'phel bar byed pa yin/_/nam gru de las rgyu skar yin/_/bra nye dang ni chu smad dang /_/gre zhes 
[\[203b.6\]]kyang ni bya ba yang /_/gsum pa yin te rgud par byed/_/chu stod mchu dang tha skar ni/_/phun sum tshogs byed nag pa dang /_/mon gru mgo ni sgrub par byed/_/byi bzhin dbo ni bde bar bsgrags/_/rnam drug grogs dang grogs mchog yin/_/khrums stod khrums 
[\[203b.7\]]smad nab so 'dir/_/rgyal zhes bya dang lha mtshams dang /_/sa ga 'dir ni smras pa yin/_/chu stod gzhi yi lha mos smras/_/tshes bzhi'i mtshams khyod mkha' la song /_/n+ya gro d+ha ni zas yin te/_/yud tsam sum cur 'gro bar byed/_/gdab mo chu yi las phyir yin/_

[\[204a.1\]]bad kan gyur nad chung bar 'gyur/_/khyod spyod skye dgu der su skye/_/chos gnas brtson thos longs spyod ldan/_/mkhas pa dag dang phrad pa yin/_/bya ba kun la mkhas pa yin/_/der skye'i rgyu skar 'dir mchu yin/_/'dir las rgyu skar tha 
[\[204a.2\]]skar yin/_/nag pa mgo dang mon gru 'phel/_/sa ga khrums stod nab so ni/_/'dir yang 'chi bar byed pa yin/_/dbo ni 'dir yang rgud par byed/_/smin drug byi bzhin gsum pa'ang yin/_/'di ni phun sum tshogs byed gre/_/bra nye chu smad gsum pa'ang 
[\[204a.3\]]yin/_/khrums dang sa ri mon gre bsgrub/_/me bzhi snar ma gro bzhin bde/_/grogs dang grogs 'bring rnam drug ste/_/nam gru rgyal dang lha mtshams skag_/snron dang 'dir ni khrums smad yin/_/chu smad drang srong kun lhas smras/_/tshes lngar bzhi bcur lnga 
[\[204a.4\]]yud 'gro/_/khyod chung bsdu byar khyod zas mngar/_/zhag bdun nad gsos skye ba bsrung /_/khyod spyod skye dgu su skye ba/_/bu ldan yin zhing nad kyang med/_/grogs mang rab tu 'byor pa ste/_/mngal du 'jug pa'ang chu stod yin/_/mon gre dang ni lag dang

[\[204a.7\]]nad gsos las kyang chung /_/'dir khyod gnas pas skye dgu skye/_/khro bar byed cing med pa yin/_/chu bran la ni bsrung bar gyis/_/mngal 'jug 'dir ni sa ri yin/_/tha skar mchu snrubs 'phel bar byed/_/chu smad ces ni bya ba dang /_/smin drug 'chi bar byed pa ste/_/
[\[204b\]]
[\[204b.1\]]dbo yang gsum par shes par bya/_/khrums smad rgyal dang lha mtshams ni/_/rgud byed lag las rgyu skar yin/_/khrums stod nab so sa ga ste/_/de dag phun sum tshogs par byed/_/snron skag nam gru bde bar byed/_/gre dang chu stod bra nye sgrub/_/gro bzhin mgo dang me bzhi dang /_/snar ma byi bzhin 
[\[204b.2\]]grogs mchog grogs/_/khrums stod drang srong gis smras khyod/_/lan chung g.yo byed yud sum cur/_/bcu bzhi tshes gling bzhi 'dir song /_/sha sran khyod zo me tog bsnams/_/der ni sems can gang dag skyes/_/gya gyu g.yo rkun blun zhing 'thab/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]srog gcod yud sum 
[\[204b.3\]]cur/_/nad pa myur du thar byed cing /_/skye dgu de dag dmag rkun dang /_/rgyud sgra rtag bsrungs sa ga yang /_/'dir mngal 'jug yin bra nye dang /_/chu stod gsum pa gre 'dir 'phel/_/snar ma byi bzhin shi sos byed/_/thams cad bya phyir nab so lag_/
[\[204b.4\]]rgud byed nam gru snron skag bzhin/_/phun tshogs khrums smad lha mtshams rgyal/_/tha skar mchu snrubs 'dir bde byed/_/smin drug chu dbo gsum pa sgrub/_/mon gru mgo dang nag pa grogs/_/grogs mchog mon gru lag sa ri/_/khrums smad nyi ma'i lha mo 
[\[204b.5\]]khyod/_/bcu gcig tshes la mkha' la song /_/yud tsam bzhi bcu rtsa lngar song /_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]sha gtor zhag bdun rims/_/khyod spyad skye dgu su skye ba/_/glu dang gar mkhas nor 'bru mang /_/rab tu rig pa'i pha rol phyin/_/'dir ni chos kyi sbyin pa byed/_/

[\[204b.7\]]byed pa chu stod gre/_/bra nye dang ni nag pa dang /_/sa ri mon gru lag yin no/_/nab so dang ni sa ga dang /_/mon gre gsum pa grogs mchog yin/_/drang srong lha mo rgyal smras khyod/_/mi yi 'jam chung bya ba'i phyir/_/bcu bzhi'i tshes yud sum cur song /_/

[\[205a.1\]]gtor ma bu ram mun pa srungs/_/zhag ni nyi shur khyod bsrung bas/_/skye dgu skye mdzangs nor 'bru ldan/_/sbyor dang rab sbyor bya ba dang /_/dbyig rnams dpag tu med pa'ang yin/_/de yi mngal 'jug snron yin te/_/me bzhi snar ma byi bzhin 
[\[205a.2\]]'phel/_/sa ri mon gru lag 'chi byed/_/las kyi rgyu skar 'dir skag yin/_/dbo dang chu smad smin drug bde/_/gro bzhin nag pa snar ma sgrub/_/chu stod gre bra nye rgud byed/_/tha skar mchu snrubs phun tshogs byed/_/nab so sa ga mon gre grogs/_/rgyal 
[\[205a.3\]]dang lha mtshams grogs mchog ste/_/khrums stod drang srong gis smras pa/_/dri za'i lha mo yud sum cur/_/tshes ni bcu bzhir khyod song shig_/myur bya'i gtor ma zan dron sha/_/'dir zhag nyi shu lnga nad yin/_/srog ldan 'bras dkar brgya yis bsrungs/_/

[\[229a.6\]]rab tu mi bzad myong /_/khyod ni klu yi skye gnas su/_/skyes nas shin tu sdug bsngal gyur/_/stong pa 'brog dgon rdzong rnams su/_/chu dang bral bar gnas pa yin/_/sems can dmyal ba rnams su khyod/_/sdug bsngal tshor ba phyi phyir myong /_/shin tu 
[\[229a.7\]]yun ring [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]dang /_/dud 'gror sdug bsngal tshor ba myong /_/su zhig tha ma'i dus tshe gnod sems byung /_/rtag tu phrag dog ser sna khros pa la/_/bsam gtan mi skyob sangs rgyas mchod pa min/_/tshul khrims mi skyob dgon par gnas pa min/_/
[\[229b\]]
[\[229b.1\]]yang de'i tshe klu'i 'khor de'i nang na klu [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]lta bu bye ba phrag nyi shu rtsa drug sngon gyi tshe rabs rjes su dran par gyur te/_skad po cher rab tu ngu zhing 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag yongs su bskyab tu gsol/_bde bar gshegs pa bdag cag 
[\[229b.2\]]yongs su bskyab tu gsol/_sngon gyi rgyal ba'i bstan pa la bdag cag gis 'dra mtshungs par las kyi sgrib pa de lta bu 'phangs lags so/_/de ltar yun ring por ngan song gsum du sdug bsngal myong lags so/_/da ltar yang klu [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]lta bu rnams kyi 
[\[229b.3\]]nang du rtag tu sdug bsngal de lta bus gzir lags te/_dper na klu [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]lta bu tshon rtsi sngon po bzhin no/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_khyod kyis chu long la de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs kyi drung du chag chag thob shig dang /_des ni khyed kyi sdug bsngal 
[\[229b.4\]]thams cad yongs su zad par 'gyur ro/_/de nas klu [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]lta bu de dag thams cad kyis lag pa gnyis kyis chu blangs pa dang /_lag na chu 'dug pa de rab tu 'bar ro/_/de nas me'i phung po shing ta la drug srid du 'phags te/_lan bdun gyi bar du de bzhin 
[\[229b.5\]]no/_/klu'i tshogs de mchog tu yid mi dga' nas mchi ma dag phyung ngo /_/thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de yang yid kun tu mi dga' nas de bzhin no/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_/ci 'dra ba yi las byas pa/_/de 'dra ba yi 'bras bu 'thob/_/
[\[229b.6\]]dge ba yin na bzang po ste/_/sdig pa byed pa'ang sdig to'o/_/de nas [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]lta bu'i klu/_/khyed kun 'di skad tshig smros shig_/bden pa gang gis ston pa ni/_/sems can kun la thugs snyoms pa/_/bden pa'i tshig de kho na yis/_/nged kyi chu 
[\[229b.7\]]ni ma 'bar cig_/klu thams cad kyis 'di skad ces smras so/_/lan brgyad pa la lag pa gnyis kyis chu blangs te bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la gtor nas nyes pa la nyes par so sor bshags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_sdig pa'i 
[\[230a\]]
[\[230a.1\]]las kyi sgrib pa yi/_/lhag ma lus pa yongs su zad/_/byams pa'i tshe na khyed cag ni/_/mi yi srid pa thob par 'gyur/_/byams pa yi ni bstan pa la/_/myur du rab tu 'byung ba thob/_/rnal 'byor la ni mngon brtson dang /_/myur 
[\[230a.2\]]du dgra bcom thob par 'gyur/_/yang de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi mthus klu'i tshogs de ji ltar lhags pa bzhin de dag thams cad kyis sngon gyi tshe rabs dang /_bdag gi las dang /_'gro ba dang /_skye ba rjes su dran te/_'khor de'i nang nas klu brgya stong phrag 
[\[230a.3\]]mang po rang gis nyes par byas pa'i sdig pa'i las kyis rab tu dus nas kha cig gis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi bstan pa la bdag cag khyim na gnas par gyur te/_nye du dang 'grogs pa'i rkyen dang /_chos mnyan 
[\[230a.4\]]pa'i rkyen gyis dge slong rnams dang lhan cig tu dge 'dun gyi mngon du gyur pa bsngos pa'i me tog dang /_'bras bu dang /_khu ba dang /_bza' ba dang /_btung ba longs spyad lags so zhes bya ba nas kha cig gis gsol pa/_bdag cag gis phyogs bzhi pa 
[\[230a.5\]]la bsngos pa longs spyad lags so zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/kha cig gis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bdag cag gis rang gis bsngos pa longs spyod lags so zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/kha cig gis gsol pa/_bdag cag de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par 
[\[230a.6\]]gzigs kyi zhes bya ba'i bar dang /_bdag cag gtsug tor can gyi zhes bya ba'i bar dang /_bdag cag 'khor ba 'jig gi zhes bya ba'i bar dang /_bdag cag gser thub kyi zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/klu kha cig gis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/de bzhin gshegs 
[\[230a.7\]]pa 'od srung gi bstan pa la bdag cag khyim na gnas par gyur te/_nye du dang 'grogs pa'i rkyen dang /_chos mnyan pa'i rkyen gyis de bzhin du bsngos pa'i dge 'dun gyi me tog dang /_'bras bu dang /_khu ba dang /_bza' ba dang /_btung ba dge slong rnams 
[\[230b\]]
[\[230b.1\]]la stsal pa de bdag cag gis longs spyad lags so/_/sdig pa'i las des kyang sems can dmyal ba rnams kyi nang du sdug bsngal mi bzad pa chen po rnam pa sna tshogs rab tu mi bzad pa mang po yang dang yang nyams su myong lags so/_/dud 'gro dang yi 
[\[230b.2\]]dgas rnams kyi nang du dus yun ring por rtag tu sdug bsngal gyis gzir lags so/_/las kyi lhag ma des bdag cag klu'i skye gnas dman pa 'dir skyes nas chab bskol ba kun tu tsha ba'i nang du btsos lags so/_/bye ma kun tu tsha ba'i nang du bsregs 
[\[230b.3\]]lags so/_/bdag cag gi kha'i sgor kha zas stsal ba yang lcags kyi thu lum du gyur lags so/_/de bzhin du khar btung ba stsal ba yang lcags dang zangs su gyur lags so/_/gang dang gang du mchis pa thams cad du sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba de lta bu myong 
[\[230b.4\]]lags na bdag cag yongs su bskyab tu gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_kye lag 'gro dag khyed kyis dge 'dun gyi longs spyod pa de ni 'os pa ma yin te/_mtshams med pa dang nye ba yin no/_/rnam par ma smin par gang gis kyang myur 
[\[230b.5\]]du bshags par mi nus so/_/kye lag 'gro dag de lta yin yang ji srid 'tsho'i bar du skyabs gsum du 'gro ba la 'khod cig dang /_de lta na khyed la phan pa dang bde bar 'gyur ro/_/rgyu des de bzhin gshegs pas khyed 'dod pa bzhin 'khor bar gzhog par mdzad 
[\[230b.6\]]do/_/de dag thams cad gsum la skyabs su 'gro ba la yang bkod par gyur to/_/yang 'khor de'i nang na klu mo long ma zhig yod de/_de'i kha srin bus gang zhing gcin rul pa lta bu'i rnag dri nga ba zag go/_/smad kyi mtshan ma la yang de bzhin no/_/de'i lus 
[\[230b.7\]]thams cad rul cing rnag 'dzag pa phol mig dang srin bus gang ba la sha sbrang dang /_sbrang bu mchu [{]rings [,]ring [}]dang /_sbrang bu gdug pa rnams kyis za bar byed do/_/de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis thugs rje chen pos gzigs te/_bka' stsal pa/_sring mo khyod kyis lus 
[\[231a\]]
[\[231a.1\]]la sdig pa'i las ni ci byas/_ji lta bu zhig_/des gsol pa/_btsun pa bcom ldan 'das bdag gi tshor ba sdug bsngal lags so/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das bdag gi tshor ba tsha ba lags te/_gang nas klu'i skye gnas dman pa 'dir skyes

[\[231b\]]
[\[231b.1\]]nad thams cad rab tu zhi bar gyur te/_gsum la skyabs su nye bar gnas par gyur to/_/yang de nyid na klu gzhan zhig lus dri nga zhing srin bus gang la srin bu ser skyas gang nas de las rnag khrag tsha zhing gor gor po zag pa/_smra mi nus pa/_mchog tu skyon du bgrang

[\[232a.1\]]kun tu gzir nas sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba myong lags so/_/yang mi dge ba'i las kyi rgyu des bdag 'di nas shi 'phos nas yang sems can dmyal ba chen po rnams kyi nang du skyes lags so/_/dge slong de yang khro ba dang ser sna'i rgyu des 'chi

[\[239a.3\]]byad sa bon gyi tshogs dang /_'byung ba'i tshogs dang /_sman dang /_nags tshal rnams yang dag par rab tu skye bar bya ba dang /_bza' ba rnam pa sna tshogs yongs su smin par bya ba dang /_dus ma yin pa'i rlung dang char pa rab tu zhi bar bya ba'i phyir drang srong thub pa'i gnas

[\[239a.6\]]du de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyan thos chos kyis gnas pa rnams gnas par gyur pa dang /_gnas par 'gyur ba nas nga'i chos kyi tshul gnas par 'gyur ba'i bar gyis gnas de dag thams cad lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyi mchod rten dag yin no/_/yang mchod

[\[244a.1\]]dang /_rgyur yang mi bzod do/_/lag 'gro'i bdag po nyi ma'i snying po 'di thos pa tsam gyi bsod nams kyi phung po yang de ltar zab pa yin no/_/gsol pa/_btsun pa bcom ldan 'das gal te da rgya mtsho chen por mi gshegs lags na bdag gis 
[\[244a.2\]]kyang nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di mnyan nas yi ger bris te bcang bar bgyi lags na rgya mtsho chen por dge ba'i chos ji snyed cig rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_lag 'gro'i bdag po gang dang gang du nyi ma'i 
[\[244a.3\]]snying po lung bstan pa chen po thams cad bris nas 'chang bar 'gyur ba de dang de nyid du phan yon bcu 'byung ngo /_/bcu gang zhe na/_khyim de dang de zhes bya ba nas yul zhes bya ba'i bar der nor dang /_'bru dang /_nye bar spyod pa dang /_longs spyod mang po dang /_rin po 
[\[244a.4\]]che rnam pa sna tshogs ma tshang ba med par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang khyim mam yul gang na nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di bris nas mchod par 'gyur ba de dang der mchod rten chen po de dag na gang dag de la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir gnas pa'i byang chub sems 
[\[244a.5\]]dpa' sems dpa' chen po bye ba phrag drug cu po de dag gis kyang blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_mchod pa bya ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba'i phyir lhags nas der 'thab pa dang /_thab mo dang /_mi ngas dang /_nad dang /_mu ge dang /_dmag tshogs gnyi 
[\[244a.6\]]ga dang /_dus ma yin pa'i rlung dang /_char pa dang /_nad rnam pa sna tshogs rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/yang de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bye ba phrag drug cu po de dag thams cad kyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
[\[244a.7\]]du bgyi lags so/_/khyim mam zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor gyi bar gang na nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di yi ger bris shing mchod par 'gyur ba der bdag cag gis 'thab pa dang /_thab mo dang /_mi ngas dang /_nad dang /_mu ge dang /_dmag tshogs gnyi ga dang 
[\[244b\]]
[\[244b.1\]]dus ma lags pa'i rlung dang /_char pa rnam pa sna tshogs rab tu zhi bar bgyi lags so/_/bka' stsal pa/_gzhan yang khyim mam zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar gang na nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di yi ger bris par 'gyur ba'i yul 
[\[244b.2\]]'khor zhes bya ba'i bar der brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_rgyal po chen po bzhi dang /_gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon nyi shu rtsa brgyad 'khor dang bcas pa dang /_lha mo chen mo dpal dang /_lha mo chen mo dbyangs can dang /_sa'i lha mo brtan ma dang /_sman gyi 
[\[244b.3\]]lha mo grub pa 'khor dang bcas pas khyim zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar der rtag par rgyun du nyin mtshan du srung bar 'gyur ro/_/yongs su skyong bar 'gyur ro/_/de'i tshe brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa zhes bya ba nas sman gyi lha mo grub pa zhes bya ba'i 
[\[244b.4\]]bar 'khor dang bcas pas kyang 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du bgyi lags so zhes bya ba nas gzhan yang zhes bya ba'i bar du ste/_khyim dang zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar gang na zhes bya ba nas khyim dang zhes 
[\[244b.5\]]bya ba nas/_yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar de dang der sangs rgyas mang po la bsnyen bkur byas pa/_dbang che bar grags pa/_dbang che bar grags pa/_sbyin pa dang /_dul ba dang /_yang dag par sdom pa phun sum tshogs pa rnams skye ba len par byed 
[\[244b.6\]]do/_/gzhan yang khyim de dang der sems can rnams 'dod pa'i yon tan lnga la ma chags pa dang /_rtag tu sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/gzhan yang snod du gyur pa'i sbyin gnas de dag nyid la mngon par dga' bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[244b.7\]]gzhan yang der rtag par rgyun du chos kyi ro rnam pa sna tshogs kyi char pas khyab par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/gzhan yang der sems can rnams rtag tu dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam yang dag par blang ba la mngon par brtson par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang der sems can 
[\[245a\]]
[\[245a.1\]]rnams rtag tu byams pa'i sems dang ldan pa dang /_snying rje'i sems dang ldan pa dang /_'dod chags dang bral ba'i sems dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang khyim dang zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar de dang de dag tu sems can 
[\[245a.2\]]rnams ngan song gsum spangs pa dang /_mtho ris su skye bar 'gyur ro/_/lag 'gro'i bdag po de la khyim dag dang zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar du nga la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di yi ger bris shing yang dang yang klog 
[\[245a.3\]]pa'i khyim zhes bya ba nas yul 'khor zhes bya ba'i bar der phan yon de dag 'byung bar 'gyur na/_gang dag 'don par byed pa dang /_chos de dag la yang dag par snang bar byed pa dang /_chos kyi gnas pas gnas par byed pa la lta smos kyang ci dgos/_bsod nams 
[\[245a.4\]]kyi phung po de ni ngas bskal pa brgyar yang bstan par sla ba ma yin te/_lag 'gro'i bdag po nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di ni de ltar zab bo/_/nyi ma'i snying po lung bstan pa chen po 'di ni de ltar yon tan che ba'o/_/de ltar phan yon che ba'o/_/bcom 
[\[245a.5\]]ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/_phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad nas lhags pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag dang /_bdud dang /_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dang /_lha ma yin dang /_
[\[245a.6\]]nam mkha' lding dang /_dri za dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po dang /_grul bum dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]dang /_sha za dang /_srul po dang /_mi dang /_mi ma yin pa de dag yi rangs shing rab tu dga' la/_dga' ba dang /_yid bde ba skyes nas bcom 
[\[245a.7\]]ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do//_//'phags pa 'dus pa chen po theg pa chen po'i mdo las nyi ma'i snying po'i le'u bcu gcig pa rdzogs so//_//'phags pa shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i sde nyi ma'i snying po zhes bya ba'i mdo rdzogs so//_//
